Having crossed over with Vajra Force,
In its center one should draw
A lotus encircled by lotuses.
In all of its directions
One should draw the lotus emblems,
Where the two lotus mudrās abide:
The very mudrā of the Sugata himself.

Their mudrās are these: hrīḥ oṃ padma tathāgato oṃ padma samatna bhadre padma bajra aṃkuśo koṣa dhāriṇī hūṃ oṃ padma rati oṃ padma duṣṭi

Likewise in the second maṇḍala,
Having crossed over with Vajra Force,
One should draw the Buddha Abhiṣeka,
With a topknot, abiding on a great lotus.
In all of its directions,
In their proper order, above and below,
One should place the supreme samayas
Endowed with lotus emblems.

Their heart mantras are these: śrīḥ oṃ bhṛkuṭī betāḍi padme hūṃ oṃ padme jvāle oṃ saumeni padmeni hūṃ oṃ padma hāsini ekedaśa bakatre diri diri i aṭṭe baṭṭe cale pracale kusuma dhare ile prabeśa siddhi me prayaccha hūṃ

Likewise in the third maṇḍala,
Having crossed over with Vajra Force,
In its center, a beautiful lotus:
There one should place the lotus mudrā.
Likewise in all the directions,
In their proper order, above and below,
Draw them, endowed with lotus emblems,
Abiding upon lotuses.

Their heart mantras are these: bhi dhīḥ jti oṃ tāre tuttāre hūṃ oṃ dhi hūṃ padma cakra śaṃkha dhāraṇī nīla kaṇṭha siddhya siddhya hūṃ oṃ paṇḍara pāsena padma saṃbhabe badha badha hūṃ

Likewise in the fourth maṇḍala,
Having crossed over with Vajra Force,
In the center of a lotus, draw a lotus,
Radiant, encircled by a garland of flames.
In all of its directions,
In their proper order, above and below,
Draw them, endowed with lotus emblems,
Abiding in the centers of lotuses.

Their heart mantras are these:

stri oṃ padma nṛtyeśvari pūjaya sarba tathāgatānāṃ nata nata huṃ oṃ abeśaya padma kabaca bandha rakramana hūṃ ham oṃ mahā pracaṇḍi padma yakṣiṇi biśvarūpa ṅhāraṇī biśva paya sarba duṣṭa khāda khāda hūṃ phaṭ oṃ padma muṣṭi a mu

Having crossed over with Vajra Force,
One draws the offerings to the buddhas:
The Lotus Hook and the other mudrās,
In short, all bearing lotus emblems.

Then their mudrās are these: oṃ padma rati pūje ho oṃ padma abhiṣeke pūje rati oṃ padma gīte pūje gi oṃ padma nṛtye pūje ho oṃ padmani puṣpa hūṃ padma kula sundari dharma āloke pūjvaya hūṃ oṃ padma gandhe hūṃ These are the offerings. oṃ padma aṃkuśa ākarṣaya mahā padma kulana hayagrība samaye hūṃ jaḥ oṃ amogha pāśa krodha samaye prabeśaya sarba satva samayaṃ hūṃ oṃ padma śvaṃkale baṃ oṃ padma gandha dhāri śrī ghraṃ ābeśaya samayaṃ śa na mu ki a

Then, having performed in full in this mudrā maṇḍala the rite of summoning and the rest, one should bring the lotus disciples in, each as is fitting, and say this: "You must not speak of this secret to anyone at all. Otherwise you would fall into hell; you would come to suffering for a whole lifetime." Then, having brought about the descent, one asks, "What appears before your eyes?" Just as the disciple answers, so will the accomplishment be. If he sees the sky as white, then he should be trained in the wisdom of the supreme accomplishment. If instead he sees yellow, he should be trained in the wisdom of gaining wealth. If he sees red, he should be trained in the wisdom of bringing others to delight. If he sees black, he should be trained in the wisdom of fierce rites. If he sees many colors, then, knowing that he is ready to be trained in the wisdom of all accomplishments, one should remove the face covering in the proper way, give rise to wisdom in him according to his capacity as a vessel, and train him in the wisdom of the mudrās. In the same way, in all the maṇḍalas, the Vajradhātu and the rest, the training in the wisdom of the mudrās according to the capacity of each vessel should be determined in just this way.

Then this is the wisdom of attaining the supreme accomplishment:

If one meditates on oneself as the great being,
The Lord Avalokiteśvara, in his various forms,
One will gain the supreme accomplishment
Of the great mudrā.
Making the samaya of the Buddha Abhiṣeka
Firm through meditative equipoise,
If one meditates on oneself in this way,
One will gain the supreme accomplishment.
If one meditates on oneself in equipoise
As Lotus, the great Lotus Sattva,
One will, by oneself, come to gain
The supreme accomplishment.
The karma mudrā of the self-born
Lord Amogheśvara,
When performed according to the rite,
Swiftly brings the supreme accomplishment.

Then their heart mantras are these: oṃ padma satva haṃ siddhya ho oṃ buddha abhiṣeka krodho haṃ siddhya ho oṃ dharma samādhi ra haṃ siddha ho oṃ amogheśvaro haṃ siddhya māṃ

Then this is the wisdom of gaining wealth:

Placing something precious within the mouth,
If one meditates on oneself
As the great mudrā of Viśveśvara,
What was one becomes a thousand.
Taking an eighth of a measure of gold,
With the supreme samaya held very firm,
If one meditates on oneself in this way,
What was one becomes a thousand.
Placing a pearl seed in the mouth,
If one meditates on oneself
As Lokeśvara,
What was one becomes a thousand.
If, with the karma mudrā, with both hands,
One takes up every kind of jewel,
And meditates on oneself in this way,
What was one becomes a thousand.

Their heart mantras are these: oṃ padma mahā ratna pradha hūṃ ja oṃ padma supradha hūṃ ja oṃ padma mukuṭa pradha hūṃ ja oṃ padma sarba ratna pradha hūṃ ja

Then this is the wisdom of bringing others to delight:

If one meditates on oneself
As the great mudrā of Viśveśvara,
And takes a lotus, whoever is present before one
Is won over by it.
With the mudrā of the great samaya,
Holding a red lotus firmly,
If one meditates on oneself in this way,
All women will be delighted.
Having meditated on oneself,
Taking a lotus just so,
And looking with the vajra gaze,
One wins over the whole world by it.
In the manner of the karma mudrā,
Holding a lotus according to the rite,
If one circles it with both hands,
All women are won over by it.

Their heart mantras are these: oṃ biśveśvara mahā padma ho oṃ padma samaya padma ho oṃ yoga padmo ho oṃ padma karma ho

As the great Lord of Various Wisdoms,
A pure lotus before oneself,
By that very lotus mudrā
His death comes in an instant.
Holding the lotus and making it firm,
Just as the excellent rite prescribes,
By that very supreme samaya
One should tear it apart with all earnestness.
To destroy the one whose name is spoken,
Joining the mudrā of meditative absorption,
And holding the lotus just so,
That act will surely be accomplished.
If one is angered and would destroy someone,
Joining the karma mudrā,
And circling and striking,
His life will burst apart.

Their heart mantras are these: oṃ mahā padma ccheda māraya ho phaṭ oṃ samaya padma sphoṭa nāśaya rata phaṭ oṃ dharma padma ccheda bināśaya pratiya phaṭ oṃ karma padma sphoṭaya jībita masya kṛta phaṭ

Then this is the wisdom of the secret mudrās of the Dharma samaya:

Upon a lotus imagine a woman,
And upon her transform oneself into a vajra.
Playing with the lotus, the supreme lotus,
In meditative equipoise one gains accomplishment.
Upon a lotus imagine a woman,
And upon her transform oneself into a buddha.
Playing, if one meditates on the crowned buddha,
Through that one accomplishes it.
Upon a lotus imagine a woman,
And upon her transform oneself into a lotus.
Playing, lotus with the supreme lotus,
One gains the pure accomplishment.
Upon a lotus imagine a woman,
And upon her transform oneself into the manifold.
Playing with the supreme manifold lotus,
One gains manifold accomplishments.

Their heart mantras are these: oṃ bajra padma saṃyoga sādhaya hrīḥ oṃ buddha makuṭa saṃyoga sādhaya śīḥ oṃ padma padma saṃyoga sādhaya dhṛ oṃ biśva padma saṃyoga sādhaya stri

Then one should be trained, as is fitting, in the wisdom of the great secret mudrās of the Lotus family.

Joining the fingertips, make a lotus.
The two index fingers of Vajracaṇḍa touch the ground,
The two tips like a jewel lotus.
The vajra binding is likewise.
Binding the vajra bond tightly,
Raise the two middle fingers like a sprout,
And bend the two index and ring fingers:
This mudrā is the steadfast one of Śākyamuni.
Place the Dharma Vajra at the heart;
Turning it, place it at the forehead;
In the manner of meditative absorption, place it in the lap;
Turning it, place it at the crown of the head.
Binding the Sovereign Lady of the Vajradhātu,
If the two middle fingers are bound
In the form of a stūpa lotus,
This is declared to be the Buddha Lotus.
From that, make the thumbs a vajra
And the two index fingers a sword and a hook:
That is the Lotus Vajradhara,
Declared to be the secret mudrā.
From that, crossing the thumbs,
Index finger firmly grasping index finger,
When they are drawn apart, this is declared Great Joy.
"Excellent!" is likewise.
From the vajra binding made firm,
Inserting the two thumbs evenly,
Bend the tips of the two index fingers,
Place the tips at the forehead, and make the middle fingers a lotus.
From that very one, at the heart,
One displays the disk of the sun;
From that very one, bind the banner,
And stretch it out fully at the nape of the neck.
Making the vajra binding firm,
Bend the tips of the two index fingers into a lotus.
Likewise the Lotus Sword:
Join them by making the index fingers a lotus.
Making the vajra binding firm,
Insert the left thumb inward:
This is the conch. Raise the thumb like a mallet,
Bend and stretch the fingers, the little fingers a lotus.
From that, closing them all,
Make the little fingers like a lotus,
And with the index finger joined to the right thumb,
Count the jewels.
Making the vajra binding firm,
Make the little fingers like a lotus,
Raise the index fingers and dance:
This is declared to be that of the Lotus Eye.
Making the vajra binding firm,
Make the little fingers like a lotus,
Bend the two index fingers tightly,
And make the two thumbs into armor.
From that, make the two thumbs into fangs,
And insert the two thumbs inward.
Next I shall fully explain
The unsurpassed supreme samayas.
Binding the vajra inward,
Making the two index fingers like a lotus,
And crossing the thumbs over each other:
This is declared to be the Secret Viśveśvara.
Likewise, from the vajra binding,
Raise the little and middle fingers evenly,
And join the thumbs as a vajra:
The Tathāgata in the center of the topknot.
Likewise, from the vajra binding,
Make the thumbs like a lotus,
As though drawing it out from the mouth,
And place it in the lap as in meditative absorption.
Likewise, from the vajra binding,
Binding the faces of all the fingers
Evenly and firmly,
The two index fingers are made even as a vajra.
Likewise, from the vajra binding,
Make the two index fingers like a lotus,
Cross the two thumbs as well,
And bend the two middle fingers with their tips joined.
Likewise, from the vajra binding,
Join the two thumbs firmly as a vajra,
Make the index fingers a sword and a hook,
And make the little fingers like a lotus.
Likewise, turning it over,
Raise the left index finger,
Join the tips of the index finger and thumb,
Hold it firmly, and act as though summoning.
Then, bending the two index fingers,
And pressing them down with the two thumbs:
This is the great-souled Lotus Delight,
Who grants the word "Excellent!"
Likewise, from the vajra binding,
Join the faces of all the fingers,
Press them down with the two thumbs,
And join the index fingers evenly.
That very one, at the heart, is made the sun;
At the crown it is made the Lotus Banner;
Lowered and placed at the cheek beside the mouth,
It is the laughter of the Lotus Great Laughter.
Binding the vajra inward,
In the manner of the Dharma Vajra,
The little fingers and thumbs made fully even:
This is the samaya of Tārā.
Likewise, from the vajra binding,
Make the index and middle fingers a lotus sword;
Likewise, inward, make a wheel.
Recitation and giving are likewise.
Likewise, from the vajra binding,
Touching thumbs and index fingers, make them two;
Then, joining them at the heart:
This is declared to be the secret protection.
Likewise, from the vajra binding,
Make the little fingers like a lotus,
Bend the two index fingers deeply,
And extend the two thumbs outward, with fangs.
From that, in the manner of a fist,
Squeezing with the sides of the two thumbs:
The great Fist of the Lotus Secret,
Fully established as the supreme samaya.
The Lotus Lāsyā and the rest,
All the others among them,
Should be bound in the same way,
Binding the vajra inward.

Next, these are the Dharma mudrās of the Lotus Secret: hrāḥ grāḥ pāḥ hrāḥ śāḥ sāḥ dhāḥ hrāḥ gāḥ dhāḥ krāḥ bāḥ dāḥ rāḥ ṣṭāḥ sāḥ

In brief, the karma mudrās are to be made with the two fists.

This completes the extensive rite of the maṇḍala of the secret Dharma mudrās, from the Great King of Ritual Manuals of the Dharma Samaya of All Tathāgatas.

Then again the Blessed One entered into the meditative absorption called "The Lotus Blessed to Arise from the Samaya of the Dharma Wisdom of All Tathāgatas," and uttered this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ dharma samādhi jñāna tathāgata hūṃ

Then the great bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi uttered this, his own Supreme Vidyā, arising from the Dharma of his own family: oṃ bajra dharma hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva mahāsattva Vajragarbha uttered this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ ratna dharma hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva mahāsattva Vajranetra uttered this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ dharma dharma hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva mahāsattva Vajraviśva uttered this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ karma dharma hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva mahāsattva, the noble Samantāvalokiteśvara, uttered this, his own Dharma maṇḍala:

Next I shall fully explain
The excellent wisdom maṇḍala,
Which resembles the Vajradhātu
And is called the Dharma Wisdom.
In the manner of the great maṇḍala,
Lay out the whole maṇḍala with the cord.
In its center one should draw
The Wisdom Vajra Tathāgata.
In all of its directions,
According to the rite, draw the great beings,
Viśveśvara and the rest,
Absorbed in meditative absorption.

Their heart mantras are these:

oṃ jñāna buddha hūṃ oṃ jñāna biśveśvare hūṃ oṃ jñān buddha mukuṭa hūṃ oṃ jñāna dharmeśvare hūṃ oṃ jñāna amogheśvare hūṃ oṃ jñana padma buddha hūṃ oṃ jvāna padma bajra ddhāra hūṃ oṃ jvāna padma māra hūṃ oṃ jvāna padma duṣṭi hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma bhṛkuṭī hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma sūrya hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma candra hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma hāsa hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma tārā hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma kumāra hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma nārāya hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma hāsa hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma nṛtyeśvara hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma rakṣa hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma yakṣa hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma muṣṭi hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma lāsye hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma māle hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma gīdye hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma nṛtye hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma dhūpe hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma puṣpe hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma dīpe hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma gandhe hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma aṃkuśa hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma amogha pāśa hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma sphoṭa hūṃ oṃ jñāna padma ābeśa hūṃ

Then, having performed in full in this Dharma maṇḍala the rite of summoning and the rest, and having brought the disciples in as is fitting, one should say this: "You must not speak of this to anyone else. Otherwise you would fall into hell and come to suffering." Then one should give rise to wisdom in him:

Absorbed in equipoise as Lokeśvara,
If one meditates on a lotus at the heart
And attains the lotus absorption,
At once one swiftly soars aloft.
Absorbed in equipoise as the Lord of the World,
If one meditates on a lotus at the forehead,
And through practice it becomes very firm,
At once one soars into the sky.
Through the absorption of Lokeśvara,
If one meditates on a lotus upon the tongue,
When it is accomplished, swiftly
One travels through the sky.
Through the absorption of Lokeśvara,
If one meditates on a lotus at the crown of the head,
When it is accomplished, swiftly,
In an instant, one soars upward.

Their heart mantras are these: oṃ padma jñāna hṛdaya ābeśa oṃ padma jñāna abhiṣeka ābeśa oṃ padma jñāna bidyottama ābeśa oṃ padma jñāna uṣṇīṣa ābeśa

In the sky or in some other direction,
If one meditates on the shape of a lotus,
And this rite is accomplished,
In an instant one becomes invisible.
In the sky or in some other direction,
If one meditates on the shape of a lotus
And meditates on oneself
Seated upon it, one becomes invisible.
In the sky or in some other direction,
Meditating on the shape of a lotus,
Whenever one sees it, take it up:
Swiftly one becomes invisible.
In the sky or in some other direction,
Meditating on the shape of a lotus,
If one eats the lotus that one sees,
At once one becomes invisible.

Their heart mantras are these: oṃ padma ākarṣa oṃ padma tha oṃ padma jñāna grahāna oṃ padma rasayana hūṃ phaṭ

Binding the great mudrā,
If one holds in the hand the shape
Of a lotus with its many colors and forms,
One will bear every form.
Drawing the shapes of many lotuses
In the colors and forms of lotuses,
If one meditates on just that,
One will come to bear many forms.
In the sky or in some other direction,
If one meditates on the colors and forms of a lotus
As many different shapes,
One will take on a form made of sound.
Making the colors and forms of a lotus
Into many different shapes,
And mounting it, one travels through the sky,
And surely takes whatever form one wishes.

Their heart mantras are these: oṃ padma dhari biśvarūpa prabartaya ābeśa oṃ biśva padma prabartaya māṃ oṃ samādhi biśva padma ābeśa oṃ biśva padma manoṭṭhipa ākaśa biśvarūpaṃ adhitiṣṭha māṃ

Binding well, through meditative absorption,
Any great mudrā at all,
If one takes up a lotus and gives it,
Without fail one brings that person under one's power.
If one binds any mudrā at all
Of those called the samaya mudrās,
And then takes up a lotus
And gives it, one brings that person under one's power.
Binding any mudrā at all
Of the virtue born from meditative absorption,
Whoever is given the wisdom lotus
Is swiftly won over by it.
Endowed with any samaya at all,
If one binds the karma mudrā,
Whoever is given a lotus
At once comes under one's power.

Their heart mantras are these: oṃ mahā jñāna padma ho oṃ samaya padma ho oṃ jñāna padma ho oṃ karma padma ho

Then one is trained in the wisdom of the great mudrās of the wisdom maṇḍala.

In the manner of the Dharma maṇḍala,
One should accomplish the great mudrā.
Next I shall fully explain:
The supreme samaya is to be accomplished,
And in the manner of the Dharma maṇḍala
The lotus of the lotus is to be placed.
Making the Dharma fist firm,
One should make the karma mudrā twofold.

This completes the extensive rite of the wisdom maṇḍala, from the Great King of Ritual Manuals of the Dharma Samaya of All Tathāgatas.

Then again the Blessed One entered into the meditative absorption called "The Lotus Blessed to Arise from the Samaya of the Dharma Karma of All Tathāgatas," and uttered this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ sarba tathāgata karma kṛ hūṃ

Then Vajrapāṇi uttered this, his own Supreme Vidyā, arising from the karma of his own family: oṃ hūṃ dhi

Then the bodhisattva Vajragarbha uttered this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ ratna karma samaye hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva Vajranetra uttered this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ padma karme hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva Vajraviśva uttered this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ biśva karme hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva, the noble Avalokiteśvara, explained this, his own karma maṇḍala:

Next I shall fully explain
The excellent karma maṇḍala,
Which resembles the Vajradhātu
And is called the Lotus Karma.
In the manner of the great maṇḍala,
Lay out the whole maṇḍala with the cord.
Draw all the buddhas in every direction,
Each holding the emblem of a lotus.

Their vidyā heart mantras are these:

oṃ puriṇi hūṃ biśva karmeśvari hūṃ oṃ tathāgateśvara abhiṣeka karma pidyā hūṃ oṃ dharma karmeśvara jñāna pūjā samaye hūṃ oṃ amogha karmeśvari hūṃ oṃ padma karma buddhe hūṃ oṃ padma karma bajriṇi huṃ oṃ padma karmiṇi māraṇa pūjā karma samaye hūṃ oṃ padma karma duṣṭi hūṃ oṃ padma karma bhṛkuṭi hūṃ tra oṃ padma karma sūrye hūṃ oṃ padma karma dhvaje hūṃ oṃ padma karma hāsa huṃ oṃ padma karma tāre hūṃ oṃ padma karma kumāri hūṃ oṃ padma karma nārāyani hūṃ oṃ padma karma bradmi hūṃ oṃ padma karma nṛteśvari hūṃ oṃ padma rakṣa rakṣa karma samye hūṃ oṃ mahā pracaṇḍa gītini padma daṃṣṭrā karma kari hūṃ oṃ padma karma muṣṭi gatāya hūṃ oṃ rati pūje hūṃ jaḥ oṃ abhiṣeka pūje hūṃ ja oṃ gīte pūje hūṃ dha oṃ nṛtye pūje hūṃ baṃ oṃ dhupe pūje hūṃ aḥ oṃ puṣpe pūje hūṃ tra oṃ āloke pūje hūṃ dhiḥ oṃ gandhe pūje hūṃ baṃ oṃ hayagrviba ānaya hūṃ ja oṃ amogha pāśa krodha pīḍaya hūṃ phaṭ oṃ padma śāṃkala bandhe hūṃ phaṭ oṃ padma ghandha ābeśaye hūṃ phaṭ

Then, having performed in full in this karma maṇḍala the rite of summoning and the rest, one should bring the disciple in as is fitting and instruct him thus: "Son of good family, you must not speak of this method to anyone. Otherwise you would fall into hell." Then one should give rise to wisdom in him.

Then one should be trained in the wisdom of confessing misdeeds:

Meditating in deep equipoise
On the great mudrā of Lokeśvara,
By confessing his misdeeds, swiftly
He is cleansed of every misdeed.
Through the absorption of Lokeśvara,
Binding the supreme samaya,
If he confesses all his misdeeds,
He is freed from the five deeds of immediate retribution.
Meditating in deep equipoise
In the absorption of Lokeśvara,
If he confesses all his misdeeds,
He utterly crushes every misdeed.
In short, binding well
Any karma mudrā at all,
If he confesses all his actions,
He is fully cleansed of all his actions.

Their heart mantras are these: oṃ sarba pāpaṃ saṃśodhana mahā padma oṃ sarba ānantatya śodhan samaya padma oṃ sarba pāpaṃ praṇaśaṇa dharma padma oṃ sarba padma ābarṇa biśodhaka karma padma

Then one is trained in the wisdom by which all obscurations are completely exhausted:

Meditating in deep equipoise
On the great mudrā of Lokeśvara,
If one says śuddhya śuddhya,
One is purified of all actions.
Through the absorption of Lokeśvara,
If one binds the karma samaya
And says buddhya buddhya,
One is purified of all actions.
Absorbed in equipoise as the Lord of the World,
Meditating as the Dharma mudrā,
If one says dhi dhi dhi dhi,
One is purified of all actions.
Through the absorption of the Lord of the World,
Binding the karma mudrā,
If one says hī hī hī hī,
One is purified of all actions.

Their heart mantras are these: oṃ pāpaṃ kṣaya padma oṃ ābarṇa kṣaya padma oṃ karma kṣaya padma

Then one is trained in the wisdom of making offerings to all the tathāgatas:

Binding in perfect equipoise
The great mudrā of Lokeśvara,
If one says oṃ oṃ oṃ oṃ,
One performs every offering.
Through the absorption of the Lord of the World,
Binding the supreme samaya,
If one says bhu bhu bhu bhu,
All the buddhas are honored by it.
Absorbed in equipoise as the Lord of the World,
Meditating on the Dharma lotus,
If one says he he he he,
All the buddhas are honored by it.
Binding in deep equipoise
The karma mudrā of the lotus,
If one says dhe dhe dhe dhe,
All the buddhas are honored by it.

Their heart mantras are these: oṃ kāra padma oṃ bhūkāra padma oṃ hekāra padma oṃ dhekāra padma oṃ nibarṇa kṣaya padma

Then one is trained in the wisdom of accomplishment:

Binding in equipoise
The great mudrā of the Lord of the World,
If one says hrī hrī hrī hrī,
One is accomplished as the Lord of the World.
Through the absorption of the Lord of the World,
Binding the one called the samaya,
If one says śrī śrī śrī śrī,
One gains the supreme accomplishment.
Meditating in equipoise
In the equipoise of the Lord of the World,
If one says dhig dhig dhig dhig,
One is accomplished as the Lotus Wrathful One.
Binding the karma mudrā
Of the virtuous Great Lotus,
If one says ssi ssi ssi ssi,
One gains every accomplishment.

Their heart mantras are these: oṃ hrīḥ sidhya oṃ śrī sidhya oṃ dhṛka siddha oṃ si siddhya

Then this is the wisdom of the secret karma mudrās:

Absorbed in equipoise as the Lord of the World,
Making love with all women,
If one says aho sukha,
All the buddhas are honored by it.
Absorbed in equipoise as the Lord of the World,
Making love with all women,
If one says priya priya,
The buddhas, the blessed ones, are delighted.
Absorbed in equipoise as the Lord of the World,
Making love with all women,
If one says aho rati,
One will always be joyful.
Absorbed in equipoise as the Lord of the World,
Making love with all women,
If one says sukha sukha,
One's bliss will never decline.

Their heart mantras are these: oṃ sarba buddha pūja prabartana padma oṃ prītikāra padma oṃ rati prabartana padma oṃ mahā sukha padma dṛḍha hūṃ

Then one should be trained in the karma mudrās as is fitting:

Then, joining the lotus palms,
Contemplating the samaya mudrā, the vajra,
In the manner of the karma maṇḍala,
Place them in all the locations.

Then the Dharma mudrā of the Lotus family is to be spoken as dra.

Making the two karma mudrās,
One accomplishes through that karma mudrā.

This completes the extensive rite of the karma maṇḍala, from the Great King of Ritual Manuals of the Dharma Samaya of All Tathāgatas.

Then again the Blessed One entered into equipoise in the samādhi called Blessing of the Samaya Mudrā of Vajradharma, and spoke this heart of the mudrā: oṃ bajri dharma padma hūṃ

Then Vajrapāṇi spoke this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ bajra jhi

Then the bodhisattva Vajragarbha spoke this, his own mudrā: oṃ bajra ratna mukuṭi hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva Vajranetra spoke this, his own mudrā: oṃ dharma padma dhi

Then the bodhisattva Vajraviśva spoke this, his own mudrā: oṃ sarba mukhe hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva mahāsattva, the noble Avalokiteśvara, spoke this maṇḍala of the four mudrās.

Next it shall be fully explained:
The supreme maṇḍala of the four mudrās
Is like the Vajradhātu,
And should be made like the great maṇḍala.
In the center of the mudrā maṇḍala
An image of the Buddha should be placed.
In all of its directions
Draw the vajra, the lotus, and the rest.

Then, having performed in this maṇḍala the rites of summoning and so forth in their extensive form, one should bring the disciple in as is proper and command him thus: "Do not tell this to anyone, lest you suffer always, and, never having cast off unhappiness, fall into hell when you die."

Then one should generate his wisdom:
Holding a lotus with the hand,
Grip it and bind it firmly.
If one offers that fragrance to the buddhas,
One will swiftly gain accomplishment.

Resting in equipoise, at the center of the crown
Place well an image of the Buddha;
If, while walking, one displays the vajra swagger,
One brings the whole world under one's power.

The lotus, the form of the Great Lotus:
Abiding in samādhi just as it is,
Whoever is struck by the mind is slain.
Wherever it strikes, it performs
The works of increase, of the earth, and the rest.

The lotus, the form of the Great Lotus:
Making the lotus into a crossed lotus
And holding it with the hands,
Whirling it, one performs the bringing down and the rest.

Then their heart mantras are these: oṃ bajra ratna hūṃ oṃ sarba tathāgata āśa paripūraṇa mahā ratna hūṃ oṃ bajra hūṃ tra oṃ gandha pūjā gri sādhaya hrīḥ oṃ padma mukuṭa tathāgata baśaṃ kuru sarbāna lokeśvara abhiṣeka samaya ho oṃ padma padma māraya sarba dra tyarthi kan samādhi jñāna dhṛk oṃ biśva padma sarba karma kāro bhāba la li lu la la li hūṃ phaṭ

Then one trains in the secret wisdom of the mudrās:
Through passion, all one's purposes
Should be accomplished; by this samaya
Even mudrās hard to accomplish
Are swiftly accomplished by the yogin.

Here the heart of its samaya is this: oṃ sādhaya padma rāga samya a

Then one should train in all the great mudrās, beginning with the great mudrā.

This completes the extensive rite of the maṇḍala of the four mudrās.

Then the bodhisattva mahāsattva, the noble Avalokiteśvara, spoke this heart mantra called Taming All Beings: oṃ sarba jagatpi nāya mahā satva a āgaccha śīghraṃ biśva rūpaṃ sandhārāya mame ca sarba sidhāya prayaccha hrīḥ

Then the bodhisattva, the noble Avalokiteśvara, spoke this maṇḍala called Taming All Beings.

Next it shall be fully explained:
The maṇḍala of taming beings:
In the manner of the great maṇḍala
The outer maṇḍala should be drawn.
Within it a lotus as well
Should likewise be drawn.
Upon it, pervading all the petals,
Manifold forms should be drawn.

Then, having brought the disciple into this maṇḍala according to the extensive rite, as is proper, and having commanded him in the same way, one trains him in the wisdom of taming all beings:
Having properly drawn the maṇḍala
Called Taming Beings,
If one meditates on the great mudrā,
One becomes like Viśvadhara.

Then one trains in the wisdom of the secret mudrā of taming beings:
In the samādhi of manifold form,
Having meditated in equipoise,
One should join the two organs;
Thereby that maṇḍala is accomplished.

Then one should train in the bindings of the mudrās, beginning with the great mudrā. The accomplishments, too, are likewise. Likewise one should also do this on cloth and the like. The other mudrās as well, in the manner of the single mudrā maṇḍala, the mudrās and the rest will also be accomplished.

Then again all the tathāgatas gathered together and gave their "Excellent!" to the bodhisattva Avalokiteśvara:
The secret of all the tathāgatas,
The full realization of the Great Vehicle,
This sūtra of the unsurpassed Vajra Vehicle
Has been well spoken.
Excellent, you Vajrasattva!
Vajraratna, you are excellent!
Vajrakarma, you are excellent!

This completes the third section, the Great King of Ritual Manuals of the Dharma Samaya of All Tathāgatas, from the Compendium of the Reality of All Tathāgatas.

The seventh fascicle.

Then again all the tathāgatas gathered together and, with these hundred and eight names, supplicated the very one who is the vajra holder of the jewel of the empowerment of all the tathāgatas, the great bodhisattva, the noble Ākāśagarbha:

Ākāśagarbha, the aim of sentient beings,
Great being of great splendor,
Supreme jewel among great jewels,
Vajraratna, homage to you!

Great jewel who bestows empowerment,
Greatly pure and greatly virtuous,
Buddha jewel, body utterly pure,
Jewel of jewels, homage to you!

Space, arisen from space,
Great light of all of space,
Of all directions of the realm of space
Supreme fulfiller of every hope, homage to you!

Jewel ūrṇā hair, source of jewels,
Ūrṇā of the buddhas, excellent Sugata,
Supreme above all jewels,
Jewel that meets every need, homage to you!

Jewel, supreme jewel, fierce jewel,
Jewel of all the tathāgatas,
Sacred jewel, great as space,
Equal to space, homage to you!

Being an ornament, greatly beautiful,
Appearing beautiful, making all most beautiful,
Pure in all aims, the aim of purity,
Practicer of giving, homage to you!

Dharma jewel, supreme in utter virtue,
Saṅgha jewel, Tathāgata,
Great empowerment, aim of the world,
Giver of aims, homage to you!

Giving gifts, supremely giving, supreme in giving,
Bestower, giving away, supreme in giving away,
The aim of all sentient beings, the very aim,
Great aim, the aim itself, homage to you!

King of intentions, of great splendor,
Whose way is the perfection of generosity,
Tathāgata, great being,
All the buddhas in one, homage to you!

Tathāgata, great jewel,
Tathāgata of great light,
Tathāgata of great glory,
Great Laughter, homage to you!

Supreme empowerment of the tathāgatas,
Great power, great pervader,
Excellent protector of the world, supreme in the world,
Sun of the world, homage to you!

Offering jewels, most supreme of all,
Jewel ornament, treasury of jewels,
Great in radiance through the radiance of jewels,
Jewel renown, homage to you!

Heap of jewels, source of jewels,
Gem, vajra, wealth and virtue,
Source of jewels, blazing with empowerment,
Jewel body, homage to you!

Greatness itself, the tree, lord of jewels,
You who fulfill every hope,
Who well delight every intention,
Heap of jewels, homage to you!

Supreme light, all-pervading self,
Supremely bestowing the supreme, greatly supreme,
The wealth of every excellence,
Vajragarbha, I pay homage to you!

These peaceful hundred and eight names:
Whoever it may be that holds them,
Empowered by all the buddhas,
In an instant attains freedom from evil.

Jewel, we beseech you:
Please explain the gathering of wealth!
Supreme empowerment of all the buddhas,
Please bring forth the certain family!

Then the bodhisattva Ākāśagarbha, having heard this command of all the tathāgatas, spoke this, his own heart mantra, called the Samaya of the Empowerment of All Tathāgatas: oṃ bajra ratna hūṃ

The tathāgata Vairocana spoke this Supreme Vidyā called the Samaya of the Gem of All Tathāgatas: oṃ sarba tathāgata āśa paripūraṇa mahā ratna hūṃ

Then the great bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this Supreme Vidyā, the arising of his own family: oṃ bajra hūṃ tra

Then the great bodhisattva Vajragarbha spoke this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ maṇi hūṃ

Then the great bodhisattva Vajranetra spoke this Supreme Vidyā, the arising of his own family: oṃ padma hrīḥ

Then the great bodhisattva Vajraviśva spoke this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ biśva ratna hūṃ

Then the great bodhisattva, the noble Ākāśagarbha, entered into equipoise in the samādhi called Jewel of the Empowerment of All Tathāgatas. The very instant he had entered into equipoise, from the hearts of all the tathāgatas there came forth rays of light like vajra gems and jewels. They illuminated all the world realms, and, having empowered all sentient beings with the empowerment of all the tathāgatas, they became one again and entered the heart of the Blessed One, the great bodhisattva Ākāśagarbha.

Then from the heart of Ākāśagarbha that very Blessed One, Vajrapāṇi, came forth, having become the forms of great bodhisattvas, their bodies adorned with arrays and ornaments such as an essence vajra blazing all around with rays of light, gems, and the empowerments of various jewels, and holding in their hands the emblem of the mudrā of the vajra gem jewel. Throughout all the world realms they delighted sentient beings with acts that cared for their wealth, such as letting fall a great rain of jewels. Then they came back again and, in all the directions around the Blessed One Vairocana, in the manner of the great maṇḍala of the Vajradhātu, they dwelt upon moon disks and uttered this inspired utterance:

How wonderful! The gathering
Of all the jewels of all the buddhas
Has, for the sake of beings,
Become the very family of Vajraratna.

Then the bodhisattva mahāsattva Ākāśagarbha, having brought forth his own family, offered this, which fulfills every intention of all the tathāgatas, and spoke this maṇḍala called Accomplishment of All Aims.

Next it shall be fully explained:
The sacred great maṇḍala,
Which is like the Vajradhātu,
Is declared to be the All-Accomplishing.
Square, with four gates,
Adorned with four porticoes,
Furnished with four cords,
It is adorned with hanging silks and garlands.
At the corners of the great maṇḍala
And within the gate enclosures,
Embellished with vajra jewels,
Lay out the outer maṇḍala with the cord.
Within it, make a seat
Arisen from vajra jewels,
And in the manner of eight pillars,
Adorned with five maṇḍalas
Blazing with the light of various jewels,
There the cord should be stretched out.
Encircled by their own mudrās,
There the buddhas should be placed.

Here their heart mantras are these: oṃ buddha ratna hūṃ oṃ bajra maṇi hūṃ oṃ bajra ratna aṃ kuru hūṃ oṃ bajra ratna padma hūṃ oṃ bajra ratna parṣa hūṃ

In the maṇḍala that fulfills all hopes,
Having crossed in with Vajra Force,
Draw Vajragarbha dwelling there
Bestowing the supreme jewel.
In all of its directions,
In due order, above and below,
Draw the great beings
Bearing the jewel mudrās.

Here their heart mantras are these: oṃ sarba artha siddhi prada hūṃ oṃ bajra maṇi cindhya hana ākāśagarbha bhagabāna siṅ dhya siddhya hūṃ oṃ ratna aṃkuśa ākarṣaya sarba arthāna samaya śīghraṃ sarba tathāgata satva manusmara hūṃ oṃ ti rāga baśi kuru sarba na kṣa śagarbha hūṃ oṃ ratna duṣṭi hūṃ

In the maṇḍala of the jewel garland,
Having crossed in with Vajra Force,
Draw well, right in the center,
The supreme one who holds the jewel garland.
In all of its directions,
In due order, above and below,
Draw the great beings
Holding gem emblems in their hands.

Their heart mantras are these:

oṃ ratna tiṣṭha tra oṃ sarba tathāgata abhiṣeka ratna māle hūṃ oṃ ratna maṇi sūrye hūṃ oṃ candra maṇi dhvaja sarbāśā paripūra ākāśagarbha hūṃ oṃ ratnāṭṭa hā sa hā sa hūṃ

In the maṇḍala of the jewel lotus,
Having crossed in with Vajra Force,
The pervader who holds the jewel lotus
Should be drawn dwelling in its center.
In all of its directions,
In due order, above and below,
Draw the great beings
Who bear gem emblems.

Here their heart mantras are these: oṃ ratna padma hūṃ oṃ he gas mādhi jñāna garbha hūṃ oṃ ratna koṣa gra hūṃ oṃ maṇi cakra prarttāya hūṃ oṃ ratna baśī hūṃ

In the maṇḍala that rains down jewels,
Having crossed in with Vajra Force,
There too draw the great being
Who lets fall a rain of jewels.
In all of its directions,
In brief, the great beings,
With mudrās in their hands
Bearing jewel emblems, according to the rite.

Here their heart mantras are these: oṃ ratna bṛṣṭi barṣaya sarba barta sambha dha bhagabān maṇi hasta hūṃ oṃ maṇi pūja samaya hūṃ oṃ maṇi bandha kabaca hūṃ oṃ maṇi daṃṣṭa karāla mahā yakṣa hara hara sarbārthān tiśaya hūṃ oṃ maṇi ratna bandha samaya hūṃ

Having crossed in with Vajra Force,
In all the directions of the corners,
In due order, above and below,
Draw the Jewel Lāsyā and the rest.

Here their heart mantras are these: oṃ ratna rati hūṃ oṃ ratna māle hūṃ oṃ ratna gīti hūṃ oṃ ratna nṛtye hūṃ

As for the sacred outer maṇḍala,
Having crossed in with Vajra Force,
In the corners of the outer maṇḍala
Draw the incense offering and the rest.

Here their heart mantras are these: oṃ ratna dhūpe ratne oṃ puṣpe maṇi oṃ ratna āloke oṃ maṇi gandhe

In the middle of the four gates
The gatekeepers should be placed.

Here their heart mantras are these: oṃ sarba ratna ākarṣa aruṇa mahā sahā bhagabāna tvaṃ ākāśagarbhaṃ codāya ākarṣaya śīghraṃ ho ja oṃ sarba ratna prabeśaya samaya prabeśaya samaya mahā maṇi rājā kulaṃ ratna pāśa hūṃ oṃ maṇi banadha hūṃ baṃ oṃ maṇi ratna ābeśa a

Then the master of the gem jewel himself enters this great maṇḍala as is proper and performs the extensive rite himself. Then, with water from a full vase blessed with jewels, he empowers the gem disciple, binds the samaya mudrā of the vajra gem, and recites this heart mantra: oṃ bajra maṇi rat samaya baṃ

Then, draping over one shoulder an upper robe of whatever color can be found, and covering the disciple's eyes with something similar, one should have him recite this heart mantra and lead him in: oṃ hūṃ maṇi rāja kulaṃ

Then, having now brought him in, one should command him thus: "Do not tell this to anyone, lest you be poor in all your lives, lest you never be freed from suffering, lest you dwell long in hell." And the samaya should be made twofold. The Full Realization of the Great Vehicle should also be recited.

Then one should ask him, with vajra words: "Where are the things wished for and the great treasures? How will they be obtained?"

Then, as soon as he has said, "May the Blessed One, the bodhisattva mahāsattva Ākāśagarbha, grant that I tell everything!", the face covering is removed, and the samaya of the empowerment of all the tathāgatas is also shown. The great maṇḍala should be shown to him all the way up to the Blessed One, the Tathāgata. Then, having made offerings according to one's means, one should accomplish all one's purposes.

Then the wisdom mudrās are these:
The great mudrā of Vajragarbha
Is bound in perfect equipoise.
When one digs for treasure, wherever
There is treasure, one will see it.
Binding the supreme samaya,
Wherever there are treasures,
If one presses the mudrā there,
Then it will come forth of itself.
If, in the samādhi of Vajragarbha,
One meditates in perfect equipoise,
Wherever the treasures lie
Will be known by the mind itself.
Through the samādhi of Vajragarbha,
Having bound the karma mudrā,
Wherever the mudrā comes to fall,
Know that treasure lies there.

Here their heart mantras are these: oṃ mahā maṇi ratna ābiśa hūṃ oṃ maṇi piṇḍa pariśaya hūṃ oṃ ratna samādhi brūhi oṃ ratna ābiśa darṣaya hūṃ

If one meditates on the great mudrā,
Wherever the body comes to turn,
That a treasure of jewels lies there
Is to be known by the samaya itself.
Having bound the supreme samaya,
Wherever it falls and becomes two,
There, of great jewels,
It should be known, a treasure lies.
Having bound the samādhi mudrā,
Wherever it falls, by itself,
The great jewel, by itself,
Declares where the treasure lies.
Having bound the karma mudrā,
When the two hands, toward each other,
Fall, and the samaya becomes bound,
That, it is taught, is where treasure lies.

Here their heart mantras are these: oṃ mahā ratna kāya darśaya ratna oṃ ākāśagarbha maṇi ratna oṃ sphoṭa sphoṭaya tra nidhi oṃ maṇi ratna jñāna svayaṃ brūhi oṃ buna samaya bandha darṣaya svāhā

Having meditated on the great mudrā,
Likewise, wherever one comes to suspect,
There a treasure that is a source of jewels
Lies, one should know by wisdom.
Wherever there is suspicion, there too
Bind the supreme samaya, most supreme;
Whenever it comes loose of itself,
There, it is taught, is treasure.
Having bound the samādhi mudrā,
Wherever one firmly suspects,
That treasure surely lies there
One should know by generating wisdom.
Wherever one suspects again and again,
The karma mudrā, according to the rite,
Bound there, if it becomes two,
One should know that treasure lies there.

Here their heart mantras are these:

oṃ nidhi jñāna ābiśa oṃ ratna samaya muñca nidhi bandhaṃ oṃ dharma ratna bruhe nidhi naṃ oṃ sarba karma nisphoṭaya darśaya nidhiḥ bandho dākṣipa

Having meditated on the great mudrā,
And searched everywhere for treasures,
Wherever it dwells, if it falls there,
There, it is taught, treasure lies.
Having bound the supreme samaya,
And searched everywhere for treasures,
Wherever it is found on examination to dwell,
There, it is taught, treasure lies.
Having bound the samādhi mudrā,
And searched everywhere for treasures,
Wherever one constantly knows it to be,
There, it is taught, treasure lies.
Having bound the karma mudrā,
And searched everywhere for treasures,
Wherever the karma mudrā
Turns around, there, it is taught, it lies.

Here their heart mantras are these: oṃ ratna parakṣi ābeśa oṃ ratna samaya hṛda darṣaya oṃ ratna parkṣi jñāna ābeśa oṃ maṇi karma brahmāya

Then one should train in the wisdom of the secret mudrās:
Joining the two organs together,
Searching everywhere for treasures,
If one meditates on the great mudrā,
When it falls, one finds the treasure.
Having bound the supreme samaya,
While sporting with a woman,
Wherever the mudrā becomes tight,
There, it is taught, treasure lies.
When the two organs are joined together,
Searching everywhere for treasures,
Having meditated on the wisdom mudrā,
The wisdom of treasure will enter.
Through the samādhi of the two organs,
Having bound the karma mudrā,
Wherever the mudrā becomes two,
There, it is taught, treasure lies.

Here their heart mantras are these: oṃ mahā maṇi saṃyoga beśa oṃ samaya saṃyoga dṛḍho me bhaba oṃ dini jñana ābeśa saṃyoga oṃ sarba karma saṃsphoṭa saṃyoga

Then one trains in all the wisdoms of the mudrās of the great Gem family.

Then this is the great mudrā:
Dwelling in the center of a moon disk,
Just as in the painted image,
If one meditates on the great mudrā,
Every supreme aim will be accomplished.

Then the actions of the great mudrās are these:
By the Buddha mudrā, buddhahood itself;
By Vajragarbha, both are fully accomplished;
By the Jewel Hook, all are invited;
By Gem Joy, one makes others rejoice;
By Gem Satisfaction, one brings delight;
By Gem Gaze, one obtains wealth;
By Gem Garland, one is empowered;
By Gem Sun, supreme splendor;
By the Wish-Fulfilling Gem, one gives as one pleases;
By Jewel Laughter, one obtains wealth;
By Jewel Dharma, one obtains the Dharma as well;
By Glory of Giving, one finds what is sacred;
By Jewel Sword, a great weapon;
By Jewel Wheel, one becomes a lord;
By Speech, one obtains the accomplishments one seeks;
By Jewel Rain, great wealth;
By Gem Offering, offering supreme itself;
By Jewel Dharma, one makes things stable;
By Jewel Fangs, one obtains wealth;
By Gem Fist, one obtains accomplishment.

Then the wisdom of the samaya mudrās of this great maṇḍala is this:
Through the mudrā of Vajraratna
One obtains empowerment from all the buddhas.
Having bound the great vajra gem,
One will accomplish Vajraratna.
From the two vajras, forming a gem,
Through Vajradhara one obtains wealth.
From that same, with the thumbs as vajra,
Joining the gem, place it at the heart.
Binding the two forefingers as a lotus gem,
Through Lokeśvara wealth will be given.
Vajrakarma bestows the banner
Of the great gem of manifold wealth.
Through the finger gem of Vajradhātu
One obtains the Buddha jewel itself.
Evening the fingers and bending them back,
With the thumbs bound as a vajra:
Arisen from the hand of Vajragarbha,
This is declared the vajra gem.
As soon as this is bound,
One obtains the great vajra gem.
Binding the two forefingers like a jewel,
Raising the middle fingers as a sprout:
As soon as this is bound,
One will obtain one's own empowerment.
Then the middle fingers, the ring fingers
And the little fingers are made into a lotus.
By this the all-seeing Avalokiteśvara,
Holding the emblem, will give wealth.
The forefingers, thumbs and little fingers
Are formed in the manner of the vajra jewel;
Join the nails of the two middle fingers,
And raise the ring fingers like sprouts.
Making the vajra binding tight,
And forming the two forefingers as a gem,
With the two thumbs extended, their face at the heart,
It bestows the accomplishment of all aims.
Having bound the vajra binding,
Form the two middle fingers as a gem:
This mudrā of the gem emblem
Fully bestows the great gem.
From that same, the two forefingers
Formed in the manner of a hook:
This is the mudrā that summons all aims,
Declared to be the Gem Jewel Hook.
Changing from that same,
Holding forefinger with forefinger,
Making the gesture of shooting an arrow,
And drawing it, one delights beings.
From that same, the forefinger and thumb,
Joined, are called "Excellent!"
Then, with the two thumbs joined,
The tips of the two forefingers joined,
The two thumbs joined in the middle,
And the two forefingers joined at their faces:
This is declared the Gem Gaze;
It steals away the objects of sight.
The garland that accomplishes all aims
Will fully bestow one's own empowerment.
From that same, at the heart, the two thumbs
Raised and joined at their faces,
Placed at the heart of the Gem Sun,
Will fully bestow great splendor.
Evening the ring fingers and raising them,
Opening them, place them on the crown:
The great vajra gem, the chief,
Fulfills every hope.
From that same, swaggering,
And circling it, laughing, about the cheeks:
This is called Jewel Rain Laughter;
By laughter all aims are accomplished.
In samādhi meditate on the fingers
Of the one who holds the gem lotus gem;
Then, released from the ends,
It fully bestows the great giving.
Drawing the two thumbs inward,
And forming the two forefingers as a gem:
This is the Gem Sword; by its might
It steals away the wealth of beings.

From the vajra binding, form a wheel:
Inserting the two thumbs inside,
And forming the two forefingers like a gem,
It brings the attainment of a universal monarch.
Making that same in the form of joined palms,
And drawing it forth from the mouth:
Having bound it, by speech as well,
By one's own command one carries off wealth.
Having bound the great vajra gem,
It lets fall in abundance a rain of jewels;
Raising all the fingers,
It lets fall a rain of wealth from the four directions.
Having bound the great vajra gem,
Dancing, and placing it at the center of the crown,
If one makes offering to all according to the rite,
All the jewels will make offering to him.
With the mudrā of Accomplishment of All Aims
One should embrace the region of the throat;
It is called the Gem Binding,
Renowned as the rite of protection.
From that same Accomplishment of All Aims,
Placed at the mouth in the manner of a yakṣa,
It is called the Gem Fangs,
Terrifying, carrying off every aim.
Making the vajra binding tight,
Bending the forefingers, with the two thumbs
Pressing them well and squeezing:
The Gem Fist obtains accomplishment.
The supreme offerings of the samayas,
In the manner of the Vajradhātu,
Furnished with gems as is proper:
This is held to be the supreme samaya.
Form the middle fingers in the manner of a gem,
Applying them to those very ones;
By the manner of the single hook and the rest,
All actions are fully accomplished.

Then the wisdom of the Dharma mudrās of the great Gem family is this: hraḥ graḥ triḥ hrīḥ śrīḥ iḥ riḥ dhriḥ diḥ dhīḥ griḥ biḥ riḥ ḍaḥ yaḥ aḥ

Making the two jewel mudrās,
One should draw the karma mudrā.

This completes the extensive rite of the great maṇḍala called Accomplishment of All Aims, from the King of Ritual Manuals of the Karma Samaya of All Tathāgatas.

Then again the Blessed One entered into equipoise in the samādhi called Jewel Blessing Arising from the Samaya of the Dhāraṇī of the Empowerment of All Tathāgatas, and spoke this, his own mudrā: oṃ bajra ratna dhūpe hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva mahāsattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this mudrā, the arising of his own family: oṃ bajra abhiṣeka māle abhiṣiñca samaye hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva mahāsattva Vajragarbha spoke this, his own mudrā: oṃ ratna abhiṣeka hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva mahāsattva Vajranetra spoke this, his own mudrā: oṃ bajra dharma adhiṣeka hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva mahāsattva Vajraviśva spoke this, his own mudrā: oṃ sarba tathāgata abhiṣeka pūja samaye hūṃ

Then the Blessed One, the bodhisattva mahāsattva, the noble Ākāśagarbha, spoke this maṇḍala of the samaya mudrās of his own family.

Next it shall be fully explained:
The sacred mudrā maṇḍala,
Which is like the Vajradhātu,
Is declared the Jewel Secret.
In the manner of the great maṇḍala
Lay out all the maṇḍalas with the cord.
In its center, as is proper,
The Buddha mudrā should be drawn:
The chief of gems, dwelling on a throne.
In front of the Vajradhātu,
The gem garland, the gem of the lotus,
And the gems encircled by gems.

Here their heart mantras are these: oṃ tra oṃ maṇi samaye hūṃ oṃ maṇi ratna abhiṣekamāle hūṃ oṃ maṇi ratna padma hūṃ oṃ maṇi ratna pṛti samaye hūṃ

In the All-Accomplishing maṇḍala,
Having crossed in with Vajra Force,
In the center, as Vajraratna,
Draw the great vajra gem.
In all of its directions,
In due order, above and below,
Their own mudrās, furnished with gems,
The jewel master himself should draw.

Here their mudrās are these: oṃ sarba artha siddhi prabeśa mahā bajri ratna samaye maṇi sarba arthāna me sādhaya dhāraṇī hūṃ oṃ maṇi ratna ākarṣaye svāhā oṃ maṇi ratna samaye aṃkuśa maṇi kulañja oṃ maṇi rāga samaye hūṃ oṃ maṇi sādhu hūṃ

Likewise in the second maṇḍala,
Having crossed in with Vajra Force,
Draw in its center that gem
Which is furnished with two eyes.
In all of its directions,
In due order, above and below,
Draw their own mudrās
Furnished with gem emblems.

Here their mudrās are these: oṃ bajra maṇi ratna netra ānaya baśi kuru sarba artha sambhaba dāna śīghraṃ tiṣṭha aṃkuśi hūṃ oṃ maṇi ratna ratna māle abhiṣiñca hūṃ oṃ maṇi ratna sūrye jvālaya sarba mahā rājini hūṃ oṃ maṇi candra dhvajāgri hūṃ oṃ maṇi hāse hāsa hūṃ

Having crossed in with Vajra Force,
The gem lotus should be drawn.
In all of its directions,
Do it in due order, above and below.

Here their heart mantras are these: oṃ maṇi samādhi padmeni hūṃ oṃ maṇi ratnādya gra samaya hūṃ oṃ maṇi samaya koṣe hūṃ oṃ maṇi samaya cakra hūṃ oṃ maṇi bhāṣāgri hūṃ

In the sacred fourth maṇḍala,
Having crossed in with Vajra Force,
The one who rains down jewels, furnished
With vajra jewels, dwelling there,
Should be drawn surrounded on all sides
By the karma mudrās, as is proper.
Those furnished with gem emblems
Do in due order, above and below.

Then their mudrās are these: oṃ ratna bṛṣṭi sādhaya mahā maṇi hūṃ oṃ mahā pūjā samaye netra ā oṃ mahā pūjā samana samaye rakṣa oṃ bajra maṇi ratna daṣṭra kārāle hara hara hūṃ oṃ maṇi samaya muṣṭi hūṃ

Then, having performed the actions in this maṇḍala of the gem secret as is proper, one should bring in one's own disciples and command them thus: "Do not speak of this way to anyone, lest you become utterly poor and, when you die, fall into hell." Having commanded them, one should generate the wisdom of one's own gem samaya.

Through the samādhi of Vajraratna
Draw a vajra jewel in the sky;
If one places it also
On one's own forehead, one will surely become a king.

Having bound the jewel vajra,
Draw the vajra jewel in the sky;
If one places it
At the site of empowerment, one will surely become a king.

Through the samādhi of Vajraratna
Draw the vajra jewel in the sky;
If one places that wisdom vajra,
One becomes a king accomplished by oneself.

Having bound the karma mudrā,
Draw the vajra jewel in the sky;
If one places it in its own place,
One becomes a king accomplished by oneself.

Here their heart mantras are these: mahā bajra ratna abhiṣiñca māṃ samaya ratna abhiṣiñca māṃ dharma ratna abhiṣiñca māṃ karma ratna abhiṣiñca māṃ

At the heart of the great mudrā
One should meditate on the vajra jewel;
Having meditated, one is consecrated with empowerment
And attains what is supremely marvelous.

Absorbed in equipoise, having meditated
On the vajra jewel at the forehead,
If one binds the jewel vajra,
One becomes king of all.

Through the samādhi of Vajraratna
Meditate on the vajra jewel upon the tongue;
One will obtain the empowerment
Of becoming a king of Dharma.

Fully absorbed in equipoise, having meditated
On the vajra jewel at the crown protuberance,
And having bound the karma mudrā,
One becomes a king who performs actions well.

Here their heart mantras are these: oṃ bajra ratna hṛdaya abhiṣiñca ho oṃ bajra ratna abhiṣeka dra oṃ bajra ratna abhiṣiñca baṃ oṃ bajra ratna uṣṇīṣa adhiṣṭhana svamāna

If one meditates oneself as the great mudrā
And also draws the vajra jewel
On cloth and the like,
One becomes a great king.

Having drawn the unsurpassed vajra jewel
On cloth and the like,
If one meditates on the sattva vajra,
One becomes lord of the samayas.

Having oneself drawn the jewel vajra
On cloth and the like,
If one meditates on the mudrā of meditative concentration,
One surely becomes lord of the Dharma.

Having drawn the vajra jewel
On cloth and the like,
If one meditates on the mudrā of actions,
One oneself becomes lord of action.

Here their heart mantras are these: bajra ratna biṃbha adhitiṣṭhi oṃ bajra ratna bibha pratitiṣṭha oṃ bajra ratna biṃbha abhiṣeka bajra ratna biṃbha kuru

Whether of gold, or else of silver,
If one wears a vajra jewel,
Or else one of some other precious substance,
And meditates on it at the heart, one receives empowerment.

If one always places on the forehead
An unsurpassed vajra jewel
Made of gold or of another precious substance,
One becomes a king of great wealth.

Having meditated on a vajra jewel
Of gold or of other precious substances,
If one also puts it into one's own mouth,
One gains mastery over speech.

If one meditates on a vajra jewel
Of gold or of other precious substances,
And meditates on it also at the crown protuberance,
One becomes lord of all actions.

Here their heart mantras are these: oṃ bajra ratna hṛdaya ābarṇa hūṃ oṃ bajra ratna abhiṣeka mahā artha pāda oṃ bajra ratna pāca pāde oṃ bajra ratna mahā karma pāde

Then one trains in the secret wisdom of the samaya mudrās of the gem family.

Meditating as the great mudrā,
Bind the great vajra gem
And insert the gem into the woman's opening (bhaga);
Thus one will obtain accomplishment.

Having inserted the gem of the supreme samaya
Into the woman's opening,
And binding the supreme samaya,
There comes the accomplishment of empowerment over all.

Bind the great vajra gem,
And the gem on which the Dharma mudrā has been meditated,
Insert it into the woman's opening;
There comes unsurpassed accomplishment.

Bind the gem of the karma mudrā;
If the karma mudrā is bound
At the woman's opening, there comes
The unsurpassed accomplishment of all actions.

Here their heart mantras are these: oṃ mahā maṇi siddhi oṃ samaya abhiṣeka siddhi oṃ dharma siddhi oṃ karma siddhi

Then the binding of the great mudrās of this maṇḍala is explained:

Bind the jewel vajra as a sprout;
The middle fingers, ring fingers and little fingers,
Having made these fingers extended,
Placed on the crown of the head, it wins the supreme accomplishment.

Having made the vajra bond,
Knot the two index fingers, and the two thumbs
And the ring fingers, turning them into a vajra;
Placed at the hair-curl between the brows, it wins empowerment.

Having formed the shape of the vajra jewel,
Join the sides of the two index fingers and the sides of the thumbs;
Turning that very one, one obtains that empowerment.

With that same one, the two thumbs and two index fingers
Bound in the manner of the Dharma Vajra
And placed at the forehead
Confer the empowerment of the great Dharma.

Having bound the karma vajra,
The garland of all empowerments,
If one turns it and places it
At the forehead, empowerment is conferred.

The mudrā of Ratnasambhava,
The samaya born of Vajradhātu, having bound it,
If one places the jewel
At the forehead, empowerment is conferred.

Crossing the two thumbs over each other,
And bending the tips
Of the two index fingers like a gem,
One obtains the great vajra gem.

Having made the vajra bond,
In the Vajra Fist the two middle fingers
Are made like a gem;
One receives the empowerment of the gem garland.

Having made the vajra bond,
Make the middle fingers like a gem,
And the index and ring fingers
Like an opened lotus: thus it is accomplished.

Bind the two middle fingers as a gem;
If all the fingers that are raised
One meditates as gems,
One's retinues will be endowed with the supreme.

Having made the middle fingers and thumbs
In the form of a vajra jewel,
The ring fingers and little fingers made even
Are placed at the mouth: this is the tongue, the giving of gems.

There, make the two index fingers a vajra;
Likewise make those two into a hook;
Index finger drawing index finger:
That itself is the full giving of joy.

Bind the two middle fingers as a gem;
Bend the two index fingers at the sides;
The two sides of the ring fingers knotted
Are covered by the two thumbs.
That very one is turned as the vajra sun;
Extend it at the top of the shoulders.
The gem banner is the ornament of the crown;
And it is made in the manner of laughter.

Having made the vajra bond,
Raise the index and ring fingers upward
And make them like a lotus;
Make the two middle fingers a gem;
From that, at the end, perform the full giving.

Join the vajra sword
And make it in the form of a vajra wheel;
Draw that very one forth from the mouth.

Make the two index fingers a jewel sprout,
Extend all the fingers,
And place them on the crown of the head:
This sends down a rain of jewels.

That same, in the manner of armor,
Making two jewels at the heart;
The two index fingers as gem-holding fangs;
Made even in the center: this is the fist.

Then one trains in the wisdom of the samaya mudrās.

If those very great mudrās
Are bound with the palms joined,
In brief, they grant the fourfold
Accomplishment of the secret of secrets.
Just as with the giving of the vajra secret,
They should not be bound at all times.

Next I shall explain the sūtra of the Dharma mudrās: traiḥ graiḥ traiḥ kraḥ khaḥ dhaiḥ kraiḥ baiḥ kraḥ raiḥ yaḥ maiḥ

The supreme fist of the secret of secrets,
If made twofold, performs all actions.

This completes the extensive rite of the secret maṇḍala of the jewel mudrā, from the Great King of Rites on the Karma Samaya of All Tathāgatas.

Then again the Blessed One entered into equipoise in the samādhi called "the blessing that arises from the samaya of knowing the empowerment of all tathāgatas," and spoke this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ sarba tathāgata abhiṣekajñānottama hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ bajra jñāna abhiṣeka samaya hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajragarbha, spoke this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ maṇi ratna abhiṣeka jñāna hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajranetra, spoke this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ dharma abhiṣeka jñāna hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajraviśva, spoke this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ sarba abhiṣeka jñān hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Ākāśagarbha, spoke this wisdom maṇḍala of his own family.

Now I shall fully explain
The supreme wisdom maṇḍala.
Like Vajradhātu,
It is called the Jewel Wisdom.
In the manner of the great maṇḍala,
Lay out the whole maṇḍala with cords;
In the manner of the Dharma maṇḍala,
One should draw the emblems of the heart.

Then he spoke these, the wisdom heart mantras of this maṇḍala: oṃ jñāna sarba siddhi jñāna hūṃ oṃ maṇi jñāna hūṃ oṃ maṇi jñāna aṃkuśa ja oṃ maṇi jñāna rāga oṃ maṇi jñāna duṣṭe oṃ maṇi jñāna duṣṭi maṇi hūṃ oṃ maṇi jñāna abhiṣeka oṃ maṇi jñāna sūrya oṃ maṇi jñāna dhvaja oṃ maṇi jñāna aṭṭāṭṭa hāsa oṃ maṇi jñāna padma hūṃ oṃ maṇi jñāna tyega oṃ maṇi jñāna koṣa oṃ jñānamṇi cakra oṃ jñāna maṇi bhāṣa oṃ jñāna maṇi ratna barṣa hūṃ oṃ maṇi jñāna nredye pūja samaye hūṃ oṃ maṇi jñāna rakṣa oṃ maṇi jñāna muṣṭi

Then again, having performed the extensive rite of the maṇḍala as is proper, one should command the disciple thus: "Do not speak of this to anyone who has not seen the Dharma, lest you die quickly without having attained accomplishment." Having commanded thus, one should generate the Dharma Wisdom of the gem family.

Having drawn Vajragarbha
Through samādhi on cloth and the like,
And having meditated, one should make supplication,
Saying, "Precious one, grant my aims!"

Having drawn Vajragarbha
Through samādhi on cloth and the like,
And having meditated, one makes supplication,
Saying in words, "Grant jewels!"

Having drawn Vajragarbha
On cloth and the like, resting in equipoise,
And meditating thoroughly, one supplicates the wisdom,
Saying, "Grant the sacred Dharma!"

Having drawn Vajragarbha
On cloth and the like, in equipoise,
And having meditated, one should supplicate for action,
Saying, "Grant sacred action!"

Then their heart mantras of accomplishment are these: oṃ dehi sarba artha bhagabān bajra garbha oṃ dehi sarba ratna bhagabāna bajra garbha oṃ dehi sādha dharmāmi bhagabān bajra garbha satva oṃ dehi sādha karma bhagabāna bajra garbha

Facing the sky, or in another direction,
Having meditated in equipoise
On Ākāśagarbha with a well-disposed mind,
One should request excellent aims.

Facing the sky, or in another direction,
Having meditated in equipoise
On Ākāśagarbha with a well-disposed mind,
One should request an accumulation of wealth.

Facing the sky, or in another direction,
Having meditated in equipoise
On Ākāśagarbha with a well-disposed mind,
One supplicates for abundance.

Facing the sky, or in another direction,
Having meditated in equipoise
On Ākāśagarbha with a well-disposed mind,
One supplicates for actions and the like.

Here their heart mantras are these: oṃ jñāna garbha dehi sarba arthana oṃ jñāna garbha dehi sarba ratna oṃ jñāna garbha dehi sarba dharmaṃ oṃ jñāna garbha dehi sarba karma māṃ

Having drawn Vajragarbha
And meditated on him fully at the heart,
When the heart throbs,
Then, being accomplished, he grants wealth.

Having drawn Vajragarbha
On the forehead and meditated on him,
When the head trembles,
By that one obtains empowerment.

Having placed Vajragarbha in the mouth
And meditated on him right there,
When he becomes active,
Then speech is accomplished.

Having placed Vajragarbha firmly
On one's own crown and meditated on him,
When he blazes,
Then one will soar into the sky.

Here their heart mantras are these:

oṃ hṛdaya garbhe dehi danaṃ oṃ ratna abhiṣeka garbha abhiṣiñcāmi oṃ bhaga garbha siddhū oṃ ratna garbhoṣṇīṣa ākāśāgaccha

Having meditated, fully absorbed in equipoise,
On Vajragarbha, the great mudrā,
Request, "Grant me accomplishment!"
Saying ili ratna, it is accomplished.

Having bound, fully absorbed in equipoise,
The samaya of Ākāśagarbha,
Supplicate for empowerment;
Saying mili ratna, one obtains it.

Having meditated, fully absorbed in equipoise,
In the samādhi of Vajragarbha,
Supplicate, "Grant me the Dharma!"
Saying cili ratna, one obtains it.

Having bound, fully absorbed in equipoise,
Ākāśagarbha, supreme in action,
Supplicate for all jewels;
Saying kili ratna, one obtains them.

Here their heart mantras are these: oṃ ili oṃ mili oṃ cili oṃ kili

Then one trains in the wisdom of the secret mudrās of the gem wisdom.

Facing the sky, or in another direction,
Having meditated on Vajragarbha,
If the two organs are joined together,
One will obtain the supreme accomplishment.

Like a drawing painted on a ground,
Having meditated on Vajragarbha,
And joining the two organs together,
One will also obtain empowerment.

Having meditated on Vajragarbha,
And joining the two organs together,
One who plays and sports with that beloved,
The whole world takes delight in him.

Having meditated on Vajragarbha
In the form of the atoms of all of space,
And joining the two organs together,
One will surely accomplish everything.

Here their heart mantras are these: ākāśa guhya jñāna samaya hūṃ bicitra guhya jñāna abhiṣiñca hūṃ priyānusmṛti jñāna guhya sarba loke rāgaya hūṃ sarba guhya jñāna sarba siddhimme prayaccha hūṃ

Then one should bind this great mudrā as is proper. The accomplishment, too, is like that.

Now one trains in the wisdom of the Dharma samaya mudrās. The vajra gem should be set out in the manner of the vajra wisdom maṇḍala. Then this is the Dharma mudrā: sā bā rā sā ra te ka hā daṃ ti he bā kan ka ra ya ma

Then, in brief, the karma mudrā should be set out according to its place.

This completes the extensive rite of the wisdom maṇḍala, from the Great King of Rites on the Karma Samaya of All Tathāgatas.

Then again the Blessed One entered into equipoise in the samādhi called "the blessing that arises from the samaya of the action of empowerment of all tathāgatas," and spoke this, his own Supreme Vidyā: sarba tathāgata karma abhiṣeka hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this, his own Supreme Vidyā that arises from action: oṃ bajra hūṃ kāra abhiṣeka

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajragarbha, spoke this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ sarba tathāgata samanta abhiṣeka hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajranetra, spoke this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ sarba dharma abhiṣeka ratna

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajraviśva, spoke this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ biśva abhiṣeka hūṃ

Then the bodhisattva, the great being, the noble Ākāśagarbha, spoke this samaya of his own family.

Now I shall fully explain
The supreme karma maṇḍala.
Like Vajradhātu,
It is called the Jewel Purpose.
In the manner of the great maṇḍala,
Lay out the whole maṇḍala with cords.
In its center, as is proper,
One should set an image of the Buddha.
In the manner of the great sattvas,
Draw the jewel offerings.

Here their mudrās are these: oṃ maṇi ratna pūjāgra oṃ sarba artha siddha bajra ratna abhiṣeka hūṃ oṃ bajra maṇi dharma samaye hūṃ oṃ maṇi ratna ākarṣa karma samaye hūṃ oṃ maṇi ratna rāga rati karma pūje prabarta oṃ maṇi ratne sādhu kāra pūja samaye hūṃ oṃ mahā maṇi ratna dṛṣṭa ākarṣa oṃ maṇi ratna māla pūje oṃ maṇi ratna sūryāloka pūje oṃ maṇi ratna dhāja pātaka pūje oṃ maṇi ratna aṭṭāṭṭā hāsa būje oṃ padma maṇi samādhi samāye hūṃ oṃ sarba dyāgānusmṛti samādhi karmma kari hūṃ oṃ maṇi tna tīkṣṇa samaye cchinda hūṃ oṃ maṇi ratna cakra samaye hūṃ oṃ maṇi ratna bhāṣe bhad bhad hūṃ oṃ maṇi ratna barśani hūṃ oṃ maṇi ratna karmiṇi hūṃ oṃ maṇi ratna kabace rakṣa māṃ oṃ maṇi ratna daṃṣṭa khāda khāda hūṃ oṃ maṇi ratna karmā muṣṭi hūṃ oṃ maṇi ratna lāsye pūjaya ho oṃ maṇi ratna māle abhiṣka pūjāya oṃ maṇi ratna gīdye pūjāya oṃ maṇi ratna nṛtye pūjāya oṃ maṇi ratna dhūpe pūjāya oṃ maṇi ratna puṣpe pūjāya oṃ maṇi ratna āloke pūjāya oṃ maṇi ratna gandhe pūjāya oṃ maṇi ratna aṃkuśa ākarṣaya jaḥ oṃ maṇi ratna pāśe hūṃ oṃ maṇi ratna sphoṭa baṃ oṃ maṇi ratna ābeśa aḥ

Then, having performed the rite in this karma maṇḍala extensively as is proper, one should bring the disciple in and command thus: "Do not speak of this to anyone, lest you be overwhelmed by the obscuration of karma and die." Having commanded thus, one should train in the wisdom of action.

Having meditated, fully absorbed in equipoise,
On Vajragarbha, the great mudrā,
If one makes offering with all offerings,
All buddhas come under one's power.

Binding any mudrā at all
Of the supreme samaya of samādhi,
If one makes offerings to all buddhas,
By that one obtains empowerment.

Having meditated, fully absorbed in equipoise,
In the samādhi of Vajragarbha,
If one makes abundant offerings to all buddhas,
One will tame the most excellent of beings.

Through the samādhi of Vajragarbha,
Having bound the karma mudrā,
If one makes offerings to all buddhas,
In an instant one obtains all aims.

Here their heart mantras are these:

oṃ ratna pūja baśi kuru oṃ ratna pūja samaya abhiṣiñca oṃ ratna pūjāya dharma nāśaya pādi oṃ ratna pūjī karma sarba arthāna me sādha

Then this is the wisdom of the secret mudrā:

Through the samādhi of Vajragarbha,
Joining the two organs together,
If one makes offerings to all buddhas,
By that the whole world is pleased.

Then, through the wisdom of the great mudrā, the supreme accomplishment will be accomplished. Then one should train in the wisdom of the samaya mudrā.

Having bound the vajra jewel,
One should place it at all the places;
In the manner of the Vajra Purpose,
Do it above and below, in due order.

Then this is the wisdom of the Dharma mudrā: tva ja ga dha tna ja tu sa rmma kṣṇa tu ṣa rmma kṣa kṣa dhi

Having made the vajra jewel twofold,
One accomplishes that karma mudrā.

This completes the extensive rite of the karma maṇḍala, from the Great King of Rites on the Karma Samaya of All Tathāgatas.

Then again the Blessed One, all the tathāgatas, entered into equipoise in the samādhi called "the jewel mudrā," and spoke this, his own Supreme Vidyā: oṃ bajra ratna trāṃ

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this, his own mudrā: oṃ bajra māle

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajragarbha, spoke this, his own mudrā: oṃ maṇi ratne

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajranetra, spoke this, his own mudrā: oṃ bajra dharma ratna

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajraviśva, spoke this, his own mudrā: oṃ biśva dṛṣṭi

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Ākāśagarbha, spoke this maṇḍala of the four mudrās of his own gem family.

Now I shall fully explain
The supreme mudrā maṇḍala:
In the manner of the four mudrās
One should fully design the maṇḍala.

Then, having brought the disciple in as is proper, one should instruct him, commanding, "Do not speak of this to anyone." Then one should generate the wisdom:

Through the samādhi of Vajraratna,
Having bound the vajra jewel,
If one places it firmly at the forehead,
One will obtain all accomplishments.

Then he spoke this, their heart mantra: oṃ bajra ratna sarba abhiṣeka sarba siddhi me prayaccha rala rala hūṃ tra

Then one should train in the secret mudrā:

Whether a husband, or else a beloved,
Be it a woman or a man,
Having brought the two foreheads together,
If they touch, accomplishment is obtained.

Here the heart mantra for accomplishing it is this: oṃ bajra ratna sakhi bidyādhāra stvaṃ prayaccha śīghraṃ abhiṣiñcaya hi ha ha ha tva

Then one trains in the four mudrās. All the accomplishments, too, are likewise.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Ākāśagarbha, spoke this heart mantra called Accomplishment of All Aims: oṃ bajra maṇi dhāra sarba artha siddhimme prayaccha ho bhagabāna bajra ratna hūṃ

Then this is the maṇḍala:

Now I shall fully explain
The supreme great maṇḍala:
The maṇḍala of accomplishment
Should be drawn as is proper.

Then here one should train in the wisdom of the secret mudrā of wisdom:

If the bliss of attachment
To the objects of desire, such as forms,
Is offered also to the buddhas,
One will obtain the accomplishment of action.

Then one should train in the wisdom of the four mudrās. Having drawn the sattva maṇḍala or the mudrā maṇḍala on cloth and the like, one should practice for accomplishment.

Then again all the tathāgatas gathered together as one and praised the bodhisattva, the great being Ākāśagarbha, with this bestowal of "Excellent!":

Excellent, you, Vajrasattva!
Vajraratna, you are excellent!
Vajradharma, you are excellent!
Vajrakarma, you are excellent!
The unsurpassed Vajra Vehicle,
The secret of all tathāgatas,
The manifest gathering of the Great Vehicle,
The unsurpassed Vajra Vehicle:
This sūtra has been well taught.

This completes the fourth section, the Great King of Rites called the Karma Samaya of All Tathāgatas, from the Compendium of the Reality of All Tathāgatas.

The eighth fascicle.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this tantra of the extensive rite of the reality of the family of all tathāgatas. Here, first of all, the tantra of the accomplishment of the supreme great mudrā is explained:

Having bound the great mudrā of the Tathāgata,
Throughout the whole expanse of space
Bless images of the Buddha,
And having made them enter one's own heart,
If one so wishes, in this very life
One will surely attain buddhahood,
The supreme and most excellent peace: practice this.
If one does not wish it, the accomplishments are as usual.

Having bound the great mudrā,
And recited the great reality word by word,
Endowed with everything,
If one meditates, "I am all,"
Then, if one so wishes, by that very self
One does that, and in this very life attains
The state of the radiant and virtuous one.
If one does not wish it, the accomplishments are as above.

Then the tantra of the accomplishment of the supreme samaya mudrā is explained:

Just as Vajradhara is accomplished,
If one meditates, "So am I,"
One obtains buddhahood and great splendor.
If one does not wish it, the accomplishments are as above.

Then the tantra of the accomplishment of the supreme Dharma mudrā is explained:

If one declares, "All phenomena
Are pure by their intrinsic nature,"
One will surely become a buddha and radiant.
If one does not wish it, the accomplishments are as above.

Then the tantra of the accomplishment of the supreme karma mudrā is explained:

If one purifies all actions
Into the complete purity of all,
One obtains radiant buddhahood; but if
One does not wish it, the accomplishments are as above.

These are the accomplishments of the supreme Tathāgata family.

Then Vajrapāṇi spoke this tantra of all the accomplishments of his own Supreme Vidyā:

Applying all accomplishments, by the Buddha's
Command, for the sake of all sentient beings,
If one practices as the great mudrā,
Buddhahood is accomplished in this very life.

Then the tantra of the accomplishment of the supreme samaya mudrā is explained.

In the manner of the great mudrā,
Just as Vajradhara is accomplished,
If one meditates, "So am I,"
In an instant one obtains great splendor.

Then the tantra of the accomplishment of the supreme Dharma mudrā is explained:

In phenomena that are without syllables
There is no elaboration at all;
One who utters this supreme Dharma
And meditates on it becomes radiant.

Then the tantra of the accomplishment of the supreme karma mudrā is explained:

Whatever actions one has done,
Whether virtuous or else nonvirtuous,
If one offers them to the Victors,
In an instant one becomes radiant.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of the supreme lotus family. Here, first, the tantra of the accomplishment of the great mudrā is explained:

Attachment is pure by its essential nature,
And non-Buddhists (tīrthikas) cannot change it;
This Dharma of attachment to it
Will be accomplished in the Great Vehicle.

Then this is the accomplishment of the supreme samaya mudrā:

In the manner of firm samādhi,
Having meditated on great loving-kindness,
If one pervades all in accordance with the rite,
In a single instant one becomes radiant.

Then the tantra of the accomplishment of the supreme Dharma mudrā is explained:

This way of declaring,
"Attachment is pure in its essential nature,"
Finds the perfection of attachment;
In an instant one becomes radiant.

Then the tantra of the accomplishment of the supreme karma mudrā is explained:

Whether by seeing me, by touching me,
By hearing me, or by remembering me,
All sentient beings
Will reach the end of all suffering.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this tantra of the supreme accomplishment of the gem family. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the supreme great mudrā:

Just like Vajragarbha,
"I have become the lord of empowerment of all buddhas":
Having meditated on what is to be meditated thus,
In an instant one becomes radiant.

Then the tantra of the accomplishment of the supreme samaya mudrā is explained:

Like Ākāśagarbha,
May I completely fulfill
All the hopes of all sentient beings!
In an instant one becomes radiant.

Then the tantra of the accomplishment of the supreme Dharma mudrā is explained:

Giving up even oneself,
Giving wealth and bringing great joy,
If one proclaims the mudrā arisen from the Dharma,
One becomes radiant in this very life.

Then the tantra of the accomplishment of the supreme karma mudrā is explained:

If, for the benefit of the poor,
One is diligent in acquiring wealth,
One becomes radiant; but if
One does not wish it, the accomplishments are as above.

This is the extensive rite for attaining the accomplishments of the supreme buddhas and bodhisattvas of the mudrās of all the families.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this tantra of the samaya accomplishment of the family of all tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the samaya accomplishment of all tathāgatas:

One who is absorbed in desire
Enjoys it through desire;
This is declared to be the wisdom samaya
Of the great mudrā of the buddhas.

Then the tantra of the samaya accomplishment of the tathāgatas is explained:

Not being free of attachment to desires:
This great and beautiful samaya
Of the pure family of the Tathāgata
Is not transgressed even by the Victors.

Then this is the tantra of the samaya accomplishment of the vajra family:

Though not angry, for the sake of beings
One fully displays the great wrathful one;
This samaya of the great vajra family
Is hard to transgress.

Here, this is the tantra of the samaya accomplishment of the lotus family:

Even having known things to be pure by nature,
One does what is needed for them;
The samaya of the great lotus family
Is hard to transgress.

Then this is the tantra of the samaya accomplishment of the gem family:

Be it little, or else much,
As much as one is able,
One should not let a day be empty of giving:
This is the samaya.

This is the tantra of the extensive rite of the samaya accomplishment of all the families.

Then the great bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the Dharma mudrā of the family of all tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the Dharma accomplishment of the tathāgatas:

If one declares, "It is renowned as the Buddha's Dharma,"
The syllables of the nature of reality
Are the great mudrā of the Buddha's Dharma,
The way of the supreme wisdom.

Here, this is the tantra of the Dharma accomplishment of the Tathāgata family:

The Dharma purified from attachment
Bestows every bliss;
This is the supreme means of accomplishment
Of the Tathāgata family.

Here, this is the tantra of the Dharma accomplishment of the vajra family:

In order to cleanse the Buddha's word
And to set sentient beings straight,
Though one is not angry with them,
Acting against vicious māras, one obtains accomplishment.

Here, this is the tantra of the Dharma accomplishment of the lotus family:
