Saying, "Just as the lotus is not stained by water,
So I am not stained by desire,"
Even if one does what is not called for,
One will not be tainted by evil.

Here, this is the tantra of the Dharma accomplishment of the gem family:

"There is no Dharma equal to giving":
One who practices and proclaims this
Has the Dharma of the great gem family.
If one does not wish it, the accomplishments are as above.

This is the tantra of the extensive rite of the Dharma accomplishment of all the families.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this tantra of the karma accomplishment of all tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the karma accomplishment of the Tathāgata:

Having tamed all sentient beings
Into buddhas and bodhisattvas
As is proper,
It grants the accomplishment of the supreme karma mudrā.

Here, this is the tantra of the karma accomplishment of the Tathāgata family:

In the manner of the four times of day,
With the four kinds of offering,
Constantly endowed, and doing them in four ways,
One should accomplish the actions.

Here, this is the tantra of the karma accomplishment of the vajra family:

In order to harm wicked beings,
One does whatever is needed.
Through this karma of the vajra family
All accomplishments are fully granted.

Here, this is the tantra of the karma accomplishment of the lotus family:

To those tormented by fear one gives fearlessness,
And likewise tames them as is fitting.
This is the karma of the lotus family;
It grants the accomplishment of buddhahood.

Here, this is the tantra of the karma accomplishment of the jewel family:

Just as with the Buddha Abhiṣeka,
Giving to embodied beings
And fulfilling all their hopes
Is accomplished for the sake of the action.

This is the extensive tantra of the ritual of the karma accomplishment of all the families.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this tantra of the wisdom of the mudrā of the nature of reality of all tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the nature of reality of all tathāgatas:

Through the samādhi of Vajrasattva
One contemplates the nature of reality of the buddhas.
This is the very buddhahood of the buddhas;
Apart from this there is no becoming a buddha.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the nature of reality of the Tathāgata family:

If one meditates on the great mudrā,
One should accomplish all accomplishments.
Through this one attains the supreme accomplishment
Of the nature of reality of the Tathāgata family.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the nature of reality of the vajra family:

In the manner of the great mudrā,
Having bound the supreme samaya,
If one accomplishes all the actions,
One will attain accomplishment through the vajra.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the nature of reality of the lotus family:

In the manner of the Dharma mudrā,
One utters the Dharma mudrā,
And by this Vajradharma is accomplished
As the nature of reality of phenomena.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the nature of reality of the jewel family:

In the manner of the karma mudrā,
Place the karma vajra at the heart.
If one meditates on these natures of reality,
One will attain the nature of reality of karma.

This is the extensive tantra of the ritual of the wisdom of the nature of reality of all the families.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of the mudrā of the wisdom of the families of all tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the tathāgatas:

Abiding in the samādhi of Vajrasattva,
In the manner of the moon and the vajra,
Just as one knows the colors of those two,
So one knows the world.
For the pure, a pure and white clear light;
For the passionate, red and orange rays stream forth.
Having known them according to their colors,
One becomes their very self.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the Tathāgata family:

Having bound the great mudrā
Of the moon maṇḍala with its light,
Just as one knows it in one's own body,
So one knows the minds of beings.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the vajra family:

In the sky or in other directions,
When the maṇḍala has radiated out,
In whatever way it is seen,
In that way one knows the mind.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the lotus family:

In the sky, on the ground, or in something minute,
If one sees a garland of letters,
Then, in short, whatever its color,
Knowing it to be so, one knows the mind thereby.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the jewel family:

Having looked upon the whole world,
However it appears,
As it is seen, in just that way
One should know the minds of beings.
Having entered into the union of those two,
Whether going or coming,
Whether acting or desiring,
As it is seen, so it is understood.
If one contemplates having entered into union,
Whatever sentient beings there may be,
Each and every one, just as they are,
All sentient beings will be understood.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajradhara. This is the extensive tantra of the ritual of the wisdom of all the families.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this extensive tantra of the ritual of the wisdom of the accomplishment of all tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the wisdom of the accomplishment of the Tathāgata:

In the manner of the blessing of the sattva,
If one meditates on oneself in the form of the Buddha,
Through that manner of wisdom
One will attain the accomplishment of buddhahood.

Here, this is the tantra of the wisdom of the accomplishment of the Tathāgata family:

In the sky or in other directions,
One sees this maṇḍala as tawny,
If the sattva itself, the mudrā itself,
Is seen directly before one.

Here, this is the tantra of the wisdom of the accomplishment of the vajra family:

If in the center of such a shape
A pale blue appears,
One attains the accomplishment of the supreme vajra family.

Here, this is the tantra of the wisdom of the accomplishment of the lotus family:

In that very one, which is like the sky,
One sees what looks like a blue lotus.
For one who knows the family of the Great Lotus,
That accomplishment comes about well.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the jewel family:

In the sky or in other directions,
If, from that very one, stainless like the sky,
One sees a maṇḍala of light rays streaming forth,
One swiftly attains accomplishment.
Vajrasattva and the other sattvas,
In the moon maṇḍala with its light,
At the time of accomplishment, in their own nature,
Are seen before oneself.

This is the extensive tantra of the ritual of the wisdom of the accomplishment of all the families.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this mudrā tantra of the wisdom of the accomplishment of the higher knowledges of the families of all tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the wisdom of the accomplishment of the higher knowledges (abhijñā) of the tathāgatas:

Abiding in the samādhi of Vajrasattva,
Meditating on the whole body as the tathāgatas
And the bodhisattvas,
One sees them likewise emerge from the body.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the higher knowledges of the Tathāgata family:

If one binds the great mudrā of Vajrasattva
In perfect equipoise,
Then by this ritual the practitioner
Will attain the five higher knowledges.

Here, this is the accomplishment of the wisdom of the divine eye.

"Having entered the great mudrā
And generated eye consciousness,
If one thinks of anything at all,
Even what is very far will be seen,"
Thus spoke the Blessed Vajrasattva.

"Having entered the great mudrā
And generated ear consciousness,
Whatever purpose one has in mind,
Even very far away one reaches it,"
Thus spoke the Blessed Vajrasattva.

Having entered the great mudrā
And generated mind consciousness,
Whatever sentient being one examines,
One will know that being's mind.

Having entered the great mudrā,
Whether oneself or any sentient being,
Having looked with the mind, where they were born
And their form, one will come to know.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajrapāṇi.

Then this is the accomplishment of knowing the ritual of miraculous powers:

Having bound the great mudrā,
Whatever miraculous powers one wishes,
Then any of them whatever
Will be seen through samādhi.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Great Awakening Mind.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the higher knowledges of the vajra family:

Having bound in perfect equipoise
The supreme Trailokyavijaya,
By this ritual the practitioner
Attains the five higher knowledges.
Becoming wrathful, one should do all that is needed
In due order, above and below.
Through the miraculous eye and the rest,
One becomes the embodiment of all the higher knowledges.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajrasattva.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the higher knowledges of the lotus family:

If one binds in perfect equipoise
The supreme mudrā of taming beings,
By this ritual the practitioner
Attains the five higher knowledges.
"In due order, above and below,
According to the ritual, one is attached with desire;
Through the divine eye and the rest,
One will attain all the higher knowledges,"
Thus spoke the Blessed Lord of the World.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the higher knowledges of the jewel family:

If one binds in perfect equipoise
The mudrā of accomplishing all aims,
By this ritual the practitioner
Attains the five higher knowledges.
In due order, above and below,
Through the divine eye and the rest,
Having made offerings to the buddhas,
By this one attains the five higher knowledges.

Thus spoke the Blessed Ākāśagarbha. This is the extensive tantra of the ritual of the wisdom of the higher knowledges of all the families.

Then the Blessed One, the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of truth of the families of all tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of truth of the tathāgatas:

With words that accord with the truth
One swears an oath,
And if one guards the great truth,
One swiftly attains buddhahood.

Thus spoke the buddhas, the blessed ones. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of truth of the Tathāgata family:

Having sworn an oath upon the samaya
That arises from the Tathāgata family,
If one guards the vajra truth,
One will attain the supreme accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajradhara. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of truth of the vajra family:

If one desires the supreme accomplishment,
It is extremely hard to go beyond this:
Swearing an oath to Vajradhara,
One should guard this truth.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed Vajradhara. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of truth of the lotus family:

If one desires the supreme accomplishment,
Swearing an oath upon the lotus Dharma
Of the supreme family of the Great Lotus,
One should guard the samaya of truth.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajradharma. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of truth of the jewel family:

Swearing an oath upon the Dharma of the buddhas,
Which is extremely hard to transgress,
If one speaks this supreme truth,
One will obtain empowerment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, Offering to the Buddhas. This is the extensive tantra of the ritual of the accomplishment of oaths of all the families.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of the reality of the samaya of all tathāgatas. Here is the tantra of the accomplishment of the reality of the samaya of all tathāgatas:

Having uttered samayastāṃ,
Thereafter, even without having accomplished them,
The mudrās of all the families
Are bound of themselves and become samaya.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Reality of the Great Samaya. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the reality of the samaya of the Tathāgata family:

If one utters surtastvaṃ,
The great self that urges that very one,
Then even without accomplishing them, all the mudrās,
If bound of themselves, will be accomplished.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Reality of the Great Samaya. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the reality of the vajra family:

By the single syllable hūṃ alone,
In short, all the mudrās,
Whether for oneself or for another,
Are bound of themselves, or may be made to bind.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the reality of the samaya of the lotus family:

If one utters sarbaśuddhā,
Whether oneself or others
Are possessed of attachment to women and the like,
In no way will it come undone.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the reality of the samaya of the jewel family:

In brief, by oṃ
All the mudrās will be accomplished,
And one will make offering to the Self-Arisen One,
The very supreme one of all the worlds.

This is the extensive tantra of the ritual of the accomplishment of the reality of the samaya of all the families.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this tantra of the mudrā of the accomplishment of all tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the Tathāgata:

Recollecting the tathāgatas
And binding the buddha mudrā,
If one also accomplishes the steadfast awakening of the buddhas,
One will swiftly succeed.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the Tathāgata family:

Having bound the great mudrā of Vajrasattva,
If one also meditates on Vajrasattva
In front of it,
Accomplishment comes very swiftly.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the vajra family:

Through the samādhi of Vajrasattva,
If one binds the supreme samaya
And meditates on Vajrasattva,
Accomplishment comes day by day.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the lotus family:

Through the samādhi of the Lord of the World,
Having bound the Dharma mudrā,
If one meditates on the Protector of the World,
One attains accomplishment day by day.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the jewel family:

Through the samādhi of Vajragarbha,
Having bound the karma mudrā,
If one meditates on Vajragarbha,
One attains accomplishment day by day.

This is the extensive tantra of the ritual of the accomplishment of all the families.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this tantra of accomplishing the accomplishments of all tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of accomplishing all the accomplishments of the Tathāgata family and the others:

Having bound all the mudrās,
In oneself and also in front,
Meditate on the recollection of the Buddha;
Then one succeeds immediately.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed One. Here, this is the tantra of accomplishing all the accomplishments of the Tathāgata family and the others:

In the manner of vajra recitation,
Having meditated, "I am desire,"
If one accomplishes all the mudrās,
All the supreme accomplishments are accomplished.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajrasattva. Here, this is the tantra of accomplishing all the accomplishments of the vajra family:

Oneself, by oneself, in the shape of a vajra,
Meditating in perfect equipoise,
If one binds all the mudrās,
One will succeed immediately.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Vajra of All Tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the whole lotus family:

Abiding in the family of the Great Lotus,
The accomplishment from which all wisdom arises,
Oneself, by oneself,
Should be meditated on in the shape of a lotus.

Thus spoke the Blessed Avalokiteśvara, who looks on all. Here, this is the tantra of accomplishing all the accomplishments of the jewel family:

If one meditates on oneself
As a precious jewel blazing with light rays,
And worships the family of the Great Jewel,
One attains the accomplishment that arises from all.

Thus spoke the Blessed Ākāśagarbha. This is the extensive tantra of the ritual of the supreme accomplishment of all the families.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of the mudrā of blessing of all tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the blessing of the Tathāgata:

Having bound in perfect equipoise
The mudrā of the Lord of Vajradhātu,
And placed it at the heart, the ūrṇā, the throat and the crown,
One is blessed by the buddhas.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed One. Here, this is the tantra of the blessing of the Tathāgata family:

Through the samādhi of Vajrasattva,
Holding the sattva vajra firmly,
If one places it at the heart, the ūrṇā, the throat
And the crown, one is blessed.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajrasattva. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the blessing of the vajra family:

Having bound in perfect equipoise
The mudrā of the vajra hūṃ,
If one places it at the heart, the ūrṇā, the throat and the crown,
One is blessed at once.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajra Lord. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the blessing of the lotus family:

Through the samādhi of the Lord of the World,
Holding the vajra lotus firmly,
One should place it at the heart, the ūrṇā, the throat and the crown
In order to bestow blessing.

Thus spoke the Blessed Avalokiteśvara, who looks on all. Here, this is the tantra of the blessing of the jewel family:

Through the samādhi of Vajragarbha,
Having bound the great vajra jewel,
One should place it at the heart, the ūrṇā, the throat and the crown
In order to bestow blessing.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajragarbha. This is the extensive tantra of the ritual of the blessing of all the families.

Then the great bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of the mudrā of empowerment of all tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the empowerment of the Tathāgata:

Having bound the jewel vajra,
If one places it firmly at the forehead,
By the Victor Vajraratna
One will be empowered.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed One. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the empowerment of the Tathāgata family:

With the buddha mudrās, the Lord of Vajradhātu and the rest,
Beginning from the front
And then on the four sides as well,
One is empowered by the garland itself.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the empowerment of the vajra family:

If one binds the garland of vajra empowerment
And places it at the forehead,
That is the garland empowerment;
By it the vajra beings are empowered.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the empowerment of the lotus family:

Having bound the Dharma Vajra,
Place it on the head and so confer empowerment;
The buddhas empower one
As Lokeśvara, lord of the world.

Thus spoke the Blessed Avalokiteśvara, who looks on all. Here, this is the tantra of the empowerment of the jewel family:

Bind the vajra jewel sprout,
Place it at the forehead, and with it confer empowerment;
The buddhas empower one
As the lord of offerings.

Thus spoke the Blessed Ākāśagarbha. This is the tantra of the accomplishment of the empowerment of all the families.

Then the great bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of the samādhi of all tathāgatas.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of samādhi of all the Tathāgata families:

In the form of all the victors,
Bind the mudrā in equipoise;
Reciting the vidyā mantra,
One swiftly attains accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed One. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of samādhi of the Tathāgata family:

In the manner of the blessing of the sattva,
And through the samādhi of Vajradharma as well,
By reciting the heart mantras, mudrās and vidyā mantras,
One swiftly succeeds.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Samādhi of the Buddhas. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of samādhi of the vajra family:

Drawing you forth from desire,
And meditating, "I am the wrathful one,"
Through the heart mantras, mudrās and vidyā mantras
One swiftly attains accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajradhara. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of samādhi of the lotus family:

Reciting the vidyā of the Lord of the World,
In the manner of pervading with loving-kindness,
If one accomplishes the heart mantra and the rest,
One swiftly attains accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed Avalokiteśvara, who looks on all. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of samādhi of the jewel family:

If one meditates in perfect equipoise
On the samādhi of all of space,
And accomplishes the heart mantras, mudrās and vidyā mantras,
One will go everywhere.

Thus spoke the Blessed Ākāśagarbha. This is the extensive tantra of the ritual of the accomplishment of samādhi of all the families.

Then the great bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of the mudrā of offering of all tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of offering of all tathāgatas:

First, in perfect equipoise,
Make offerings of incense and the rest;
Then, if one who desires accomplishment
Practices, one will attain accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed One. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of offering of the Tathāgata family:

With the four kinds of secret offering,
Uttering the secret offerings,
If one makes offerings such as the offering of oneself,
One will succeed.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajrasattva. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of offering of the vajra family:

The wrathful vajra great offering:
Uttering the wrathful secret,
If one forms the wrathful fist,
One's own family is swiftly accomplished.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajradhara. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of offering of the lotus family:

Having well expounded the ways
Of the profound and vast sūtras,
If one offers them with mind and speech,
One swiftly attains accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed Avalokiteśvara, who looks on all. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of offering of the jewel family:

Making offering with royal offerings,
With parasols, pennants and victory banners,
If one gives every kind of gift,
One will attain the jewel family.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajragarbha.

Endowed with blessing and the rest upon oneself,
One becomes like Vajrasattva.
Through the four kinds of miraculous display,
Through the samādhi of Vajraviśva,
All accomplishments will be accomplished.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajrasattva. This is the extensive tantra of the rituals of blessing, empowerment, samādhi and offering of all the families.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the higher knowledges of all tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the higher knowledges of the Tathāgata:

The samādhi of the Buddha in the body:
This is the higher knowledge of the Sugata himself.
Having fully realized it,
One attains the accomplishment of buddhahood.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed One. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the higher knowledges of the Tathāgata family:

Having meditated in perfect equipoise
On the higher knowledges, the divine eye and the rest,
If the five higher knowledges become one's own,
One will attain the supreme accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajrasattva. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the higher knowledges of the vajra family:

Having generated the wrathful higher knowledge,
If one meditates in perfect equipoise,
The five higher knowledges become one's own,
And one will attain the supreme accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajradhara. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the higher knowledges of the lotus family:

Having generated the higher knowledge of desire,
If one meditates in perfect equipoise,
The five higher knowledges become one's own,
And one will attain the pure accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajranetra. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the higher knowledges of the jewel family:

Having generated the higher knowledge of offering,
If one meditates in perfect equipoise,
The five higher knowledges too become one's own,
And one becomes supreme among all accomplishments.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajradhara. This is the extensive tantra of the ritual of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the higher knowledges of all the families.

Then the great bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of awakening of the families of all tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of awakening of the Tathāgata family:

Abiding in the samādhi of Vajrasattva,
Recollecting the buddhas oneself,
If one meditates on this wisdom of the buddhas' awakening,
One will attain accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed One. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of great awakening of the Tathāgata family:

Abiding in the samādhi of Vajrasattva,
Meditating on the great mudrā,
If one meditates on this wisdom of great awakening,
One will attain accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Great Bodhisattva.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of great awakening of the vajra family:

Abiding in the samādhi of the king of the wrathful ones,
Binding the supreme samaya with the hands,
If one meditates on this wisdom of great awakening,
One will attain accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajradhara. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of great awakening of the lotus family:

Abiding in the samādhi of Lokeśvara,
Likewise reciting the Dharma mudrā,
If one meditates on this wisdom of great awakening,
One will attain accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed Avalokiteśvara, who looks on all. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of great awakening of the jewel family:

Abiding in the samādhi of Vajragarbha,
Performing actions well with the karma mudrā,
If one meditates on this wisdom of great awakening,
One will attain accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed Ākāśagarbha. This is the extensive tantra of the ritual of the accomplishment of the wisdom of great awakening of all the families.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of pleasing all tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of pleasing the Tathāgata family:

For the sake of one's own awakening as a buddha,
Fully working for the benefit of sentient beings,
And having recollected the buddhas,
One pleases all the buddhas.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajrarāga. Here, this is the tantra of the secret accomplishment of pleasing of the Tathāgata family:

"Just as one comes to possess the objects of the senses,
So one pleases all the buddhas":
Meditating thus on Vajrasattva,
If one urges that very one, swiftly
One accomplishes the pleasing thereby.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajrasattva. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of pleasing of the vajra family:

In order to guard the Buddha's command
And to carry out the benefit of sentient beings,
If one performs fierce rites with ferocity,
The vajra ones are pleased.

Thus spoke the Blessed Trailokyavijaya. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of pleasing of the lotus family:

Those who look upon all with passion,
Who speak the Dharma with love and compassion,
And who give fearlessness to all,
Please all the buddhas.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajranetra. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of pleasing of the jewel family:

Fully granting empowerment,
Fully giving away accumulated wealth,
And applying that too to the aims of the buddhas:
This pleases the buddhas.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the noble Ākāśagarbha. This is the extensive tantra of the ritual of the accomplishment of pleasing of all the families.

Then the great bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of bringing under control of the families of all tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of bringing under control of the Tathāgata family:

Thus, giving pure bliss,
One should not despise desire;
Striving for the benefit of all sentient beings:
This brings the buddhas under control.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed One. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of bringing under control of the Tathāgata family:

Being the very nature of the bliss of enjoying desire,
Saying suratastvaṃ,
If one accomplishes Vajrasattva
And urges that very one, he comes under control.

Thus spoke the Blessed Samantabhadra. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of bringing under control of the vajra family:

For the sake of the Buddha's command, of beings
To be purified, of giving refuge to the embodied,
And of protecting the Buddha's teaching,
Even by killing, they are brought under control.

Thus spoke the Blessed One called Vajra hūṃ. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of bringing under control of the lotus family:

Opening the petals of the lotus,
Conceiving amid the passions,
Becoming attached in order to tame,
The one who desires is brought under control.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the noble Samantāvalokiteśvara. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of bringing under control of the jewel family:

In order to empower all the buddhas,
If day by day one places the vajra jewel
On the crown of the head and confers empowerment,
All the buddhas are brought under control.

Thus spoke the Blessed Ākāśagarbha. This is the extensive tantra of the ritual of the accomplishment of bringing under control of all the families.

Then the great bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this tantra of the secret accomplishment of all tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the secret accomplishment of all tathāgatas:

Those who do not see the benefit of all beings
And have not trained in the reality of the Buddha:
If one kills them with mind, speech and body,
One will attain accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, all the tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the secret accomplishment of the Tathāgata family:

Those who have not attained any accomplishment
And do not strive for the families either:
Having fully examined them with the vajra,
If one kills them, one swiftly attains accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajrapāṇi. Here, this is the tantra of the secret accomplishment of the vajra family:

The wicked, untamed and without wrath,
Who likewise do not tame sentient beings,
And who bring suffering upon themselves:
With hūṃ one should deal with them in secret.

Thus spoke the Blessed Vajradhara. Here, this is the tantra of the secret accomplishment of the lotus family:

Those without compassion and without love,
Who do not purify wicked beings,
And are deceived by their own selves:
If one kills them, one will attain accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the noble Samantāvalokiteśvara. Here, this is the tantra of the secret accomplishment of the jewel family:

Beings who do not give, who have no wealth of their own,
Who squander wealth and cause harm,
And who have become paupers:
If one kills them, one will attain accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Fulfiller of All Hopes.

If some, not having attained the yoga of the buddhas,
Cause killing to be done,
Then, in order to set them straight,
I shall bestow my own accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the noble Samantabhadra. This is the extensive tantra of the rite of the secret accomplishment of all the families.

Then the Blessed One, the Great Bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi, spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of the protection mudrā of all the tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the protection of the Tathāgata:

Never forsaking any sentient being,
Being one whose very nature is ever to worship the buddhas,
Always keeping the buddhas in mind:
This is the supreme and wondrous protection.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed One. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the protection of the Tathāgata family:

If one utters merely the name of Vajrasattva,
Even only once,
This is the great and constant protection;
In an instant one will attain accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajradhara. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the protection of the vajra family:

Never content with the families and the tantras,
Devoted to Vajra Hūṃ,
Whose very nature is Trailokyavijaya:
This protects oneself and others.

Thus spoke the Blessed One called Vajra Hūṃ. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the protection of the lotus family:

Pure of attachment and great in loving-kindness,
Granting fearlessness to all,
Reciting the name of Lokeśvara:
This protection is greatly wondrous.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajradharma. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the protection of the jewel family:

Applying oneself, as far as one is able,
To giving to the buddhas and to sentient beings,
And never letting a day pass empty:
This protection is supreme and wondrous.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajrarakṣa. This is the extensive tantra of the rite of the accomplishment of the protection of all the families.

Then the Great Bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi said this to the Blessed One: "Blessed One, please accept this tantra of the family of all the tathāgatas, through which all sentient beings, having become masters of all the ritual procedures, will gain realization, so that they may swiftly awaken fully into unsurpassed, perfect and complete awakening."

Then the Blessed One gave his "Excellent!" to the Great Bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi: "Because Vajrapāṇi has spoken well, I have accepted it and blessed it. Excellent, excellent!"

Then again all the tathāgatas gathered together as one and gave their "Excellent!" to the Great Bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi:

The unsurpassed Vajra Vehicle,
The secret of all the tathāgatas,
This sūtra in which the Great Vehicle is gathered,
Has been well spoken.
Excellent are you, Vajrasattva!
Vajraratna, excellent are you!
Vajradharma, excellent are you!
Vajrakarma, excellent are you!

From the Compendium of the Reality of All Tathāgatas, the extensive tantra of the rite of the means of all the ritual procedures is complete.

Then the Great Bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this tantra of the secret accomplishment of all the tathāgatas. Here, first, this is the secret tantra of the supreme accomplishment of all the tathāgatas:

If one meditates, in perfect equipoise,
On the absorption of the awakening of the buddhas,
Awakening will be attained through bliss.
Having reflected on it, one should realize it.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, all the tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the secret supreme accomplishment of the family of all the tathāgatas:

Binding the sattva vajra at the heart,
And saying, "My wife, you who are beloved,
Be steadfast!",
One should accomplish all the mudrās.
By the way of this rite,
Even if one's own rites have not succeeded,
If one says, "Secret wife,"
One will attain accomplishment completely.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Sattvavajra. Here, this is the tantra of the secret supreme accomplishment of the vajra family:

If, even after practicing exactly as prescribed,
One has not attained accomplishment,
Then if one slays with the mudrā of hūṃ,
In terror it will grant accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One called the Hūṃ of All Tathāgatas. Here, this is the secret tantra of the supreme accomplishment of the lotus family:

Binding the mudrā of absorption,
And gazing with the vajra gaze,
One will attain the accomplishment of the beloved,
Whether one's own wife or another's wife.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Lotus Desire. Here, this is the secret tantra of the supreme accomplishment of the jewel family:

Joining the two organs together,
And saying, "Supreme bliss!",
If one offers the organ itself to all the buddhas,
Accomplishment will come swiftly.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Jewel Desire. This is the extensive tantra of the rite of the secret accomplishment of all the supreme families.

Then the Great Bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this tantra of the secret accomplishment of the commitment of all the tathāgatas. Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment of the commitment of the Tathāgata family:

Saying samaya stvaṃ,
And taking passionate delight in all women,
If one never wearies in working for the benefit of beings,
The buddhas will surely be pleased by that.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vairocana. Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment of the commitment of the Tathāgata family:

One should not despise desire.
Sporting with all women:
This is the secret commitment of the vajra holders;
If one keeps it, one will succeed.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vairocana. Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment of the commitment of the vajra family:

The slayer slays the whole world,
Delighting in the commitment of body and speech;
Having thoroughly purified the world,
He bestows the supreme accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Trailokyavijaya. Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment of the commitment of the lotus family:

Of pure nature, with attachment purified,
Amid the impure yogas of the non-Buddhists (tīrthikas),
If one keeps the commitment of pure nature,
One will attain accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, Avalokiteśvara, who looks on all.

Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment of the commitment of the jewel family:

Through the absorption of Vajragarbha,
Having first bound the vajra jewel,
If one steals the wealth of the wicked,
The accomplishment of the commitment will be granted.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajraratna. This is the extensive tantra of the rite of the accomplishment of all the families.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this secret tantra of the Dharma accomplishment of all the tathāgatas. Here, this is the secret tantra of the Dharma accomplishment of the Tathāgata:

Having meditated in perfect equipoise
On phenomena as pure by their intrinsic nature,
And having done all that needs to be done,
One attains the accomplishment of buddhahood.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajradhātu. Here, this is the secret tantra of the Dharma accomplishment of the Tathāgata family:

Being passionate toward all sentient beings,
Never growing weary of the objects of the senses,
The purity of delight in women
Fully grants the accomplishment of the secret Dharma.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajraguhya. Here, this is the secret tantra of the Dharma accomplishment of the vajra family:

Through the practice of hūṃ in the ear,
Abiding in the absorption of the subtle vajra,
Saying, "Kill this unfit yogin!",
One fells him with a blazing vajra.
This grants the supreme accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Supreme Commitment. Here, this is the secret tantra of the Dharma accomplishment of the lotus family:

By the method of the subtle vajra,
Through the absorption of Vajradharma,
Sporting with all women,
One will attain the accomplishment of Dharma.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, Avalokiteśvara, who looks on all. Here, this is the secret tantra of the Dharma accomplishment of the jewel family:

Joining the two organs together,
And having fulfilled all hopes,
One will attain the unsurpassed accomplishment
Of a woman, or of the beloved.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajraratna. This is the secret tantra of the Dharma accomplishment of all the families.

Then the Great Bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this secret tantra of the karma accomplishment of all the tathāgatas. Here, this is the secret tantra of the karma accomplishment of the Tathāgata:

Through the absorption of Vajrasattva,
Meditating on the recollection of the buddhas,
One should pour it into the hollow of a cast image of a woman;
Women will then come under one's power.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vairocana. Here, this is the secret tantra of the karma accomplishment of the Tathāgata family:

Abiding in the absorption of Vajrasattva,
Entering likewise into the hollow,
And thoroughly pervading the woman's body,
Even one with no inclination will be gladdened.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajra Delight. Here, this is the secret tantra of the karma accomplishment of the vajra family:

Having bound the mudrā of Vajra Hūṃ
In perfect equipoise,
If, wrathful, one enters the hollow,
One should bring her down with the mind.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajrasamaya. Here, this is the secret tantra of the karma accomplishment of the lotus family:

Having bound the mudrā of Dharma action,
Through the absorption of the lotus,
If one places it in the hollow, women,
All of them, will be protected.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajrapadma. Here, this is the secret tantra of the karma accomplishment of the jewel family:

Through the absorption of Vajraratna,
Binding the karma vajra into a jewel,
If one places it in the hollow, women
Will be brought down right there and will dance.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajraratna. This is the extensive tantra of the rite of the secret karma accomplishment of all the families.

Then the Great Bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this secret tantra of the accomplishment of the pure maṇḍala of all the tathāgatas. Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment of the pure wheel of all the tathāgatas:

In the likeness of the wheel of Dharma,
One should draw the secret maṇḍala,
And have it encircled by mudrā women;
With that, one should set the buddhas in place.
Having entered that secret maṇḍala,
One speaks to the buddhas themselves:
"This is the consort of the All-Pervading;
Through giving all, I and the rest."
To the mudrās of all the families,
And in all their modes, one speaks in the same way.
One will then attain the secret accomplishment
Of the buddhas, which has no equal.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed One. Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment of all the maṇḍalas of the Tathāgata family:

In the likeness of the Vajradhātu,
One should draw the maṇḍala well.
Seeking the supreme of all
The families of the Tathāgata,
And having entered the maṇḍala,
One who desires accomplishment should swiftly
Say surata samaya stvaṃ ho
vajra satva ādya siddhya māṃ,
And recite this heart mantra.
If one practices it, all will succeed.
By the way of urging it on,
It is pleased, and success comes swiftly.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajrasattva. Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment of all the maṇḍalas of the vajra family:

In the likeness of Trailokyavijaya,
For entering all the maṇḍalas
Of all the vajra families,
The wise one should draw them well.
Having entered there, swiftly
One makes the request for accomplishment,
Saying, "You arise from desire."
Having said this, one succeeds in everything.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajra Hūṃ. Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment of all the maṇḍalas of the lotus family:

In the manner of taming beings,
For the whole of the lotus family,
The wise one should draw the maṇḍala.
Having entered there, one should say:
"Desire is the Dharma, great lotus;
Pervading me, swiftly grant me accomplishment."
Having said this, by that
One attains the accomplishment of all the maṇḍalas.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Lord of the World.

Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment of all the maṇḍalas of the jewel family:

For the families of the great jewel,
If one draws the maṇḍala well
In the manner of Sarvārthasiddhi, the accomplishment of all aims,
Then, having entered, one should say this:
"Of the accomplishments of every intention,
The intention of desire is the supreme accomplishment.
Succeed, succeed, great being!
Blessed One, accomplisher of all!"
Having said this and entered all the maṇḍalas,
One will attain the great accomplishment
Of the unsurpassed secret offering.
From then on, if one practices
With oneself as the accomplishment, accomplishment will be achieved.
If at some time it is not swiftly achieved,
Then if one urges it on, it will be achieved.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajrasattva. This is the extensive tantra of the rite of the secret accomplishment of the maṇḍalas of all the families.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this tantra of the secret accomplishment of all the mudrās of all the tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of accomplishment, the secret tantra of the mudrā of the Tathāgata:

"Great goddess of the Tathāgata,
Beloved one, giver of every bliss!"
This is said with true words born of truth,
When, though one has practiced, she has not been accomplished.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed One. Here, this is the tantra of the secret accomplishment of the great mudrā of the Tathāgata family:

Above and below, in due order,
Having bound the great mudrā,
Say this in the vajra way,
Which is the secret of reality itself:
"I am Vajrasattva himself;
You, my wife, abide in my heart.
Embracing my whole body,
Move with the grace of the vajra!"
The great goddess of vajra grace,
Urged on in this way, becomes passionate;
Becoming steadfast throughout the whole body,
She brings accomplishment swiftly, just as it should be.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Lord of Vajra Grace. Here, this is the tantra of the secret accomplishment of the commitment of the vajra family:

"The accomplishment of commitment is accomplished:
I am the commitment that delights you."
Urged in this way, if she swiftly becomes passionate,
It will be accomplished completely.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajrasamaya. Here, this is the tantra of the secret accomplishment of the Dharma mudrā of the lotus family:

"Being of realization, you of great realization,
My wife, be greatly delighted!"
If one urges her in this way, swiftly
She becomes passionate, and it is accomplished.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajradharma. Here, this is the tantra of the secret accomplishment of the karma mudrā of the jewel family:

"You, wife who performs every action,
Vajra lady, bring me success!"
Urged on swiftly by the vajra holder,
She becomes passionate, and it is accomplished.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Ākāśagarbha. This is the extensive tantra of the rite of the secret accomplishment of the mudrās of all the families.

Then the Great Bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this tantra of the secret accomplishment of the commitment of all the tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the secret accomplishment of the commitment of all the tathāgatas:

In order to benefit the non-Buddhists,
In the manner of a painted mudrā,
If one knows how to keep the consort of the buddhas well hidden,
One will succeed completely.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Commitment of All Tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the secret accomplishment of the commitment of the Tathāgata family:

Pervading the minds of all sentient beings,
Granting bliss to all sentient beings,
Being the very father of all sentient beings:
This is the supreme commitment, the supreme of desires.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, all the tathāgatas.

This is the supreme secret of all the buddhas,
Greatly wondrous. If one with faith
Comes to know it and places faith in it,
Even what is hard to accomplish will be accomplished.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajradhara. Here, this is the tantra of the secret accomplishment of the commitment of the vajra family:

Free of attachment, with attachment purified,
Grieving at heart for the sake of beings
Who have taken up the views of non-Buddhists
And fallen into the ravine of wrong views,
In order to establish them in the Great Vehicle,
The pledge to kill is supreme.
If one knows it in this way, one will succeed.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajradhara. Here, this is the tantra of the secret accomplishment of the commitment of the lotus family:

In order to overcome the non-Buddhists:
Everything arises from the great elements,
So why call anything impure?
One will surely attain accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajranetra. Here, this is the tantra of the secret accomplishment of the commitment of the jewel family:

For the sake of passion for women,
One should always gather jewels well.
Thinking, "Woman is the unsurpassed jewel,"
One will accomplish it.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, He Who Acts on the Blessed One's Command. This is the extensive tantra of the rite of the secret accomplishment of the commitment of all the families.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this secret tantra of the accomplishment of the offering of all the tathāgatas. Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment of the offering of the Tathāgata family:

Encircled by passionate women,
The bliss of being pressed upon from every side,
If one offers it in worship of the buddhas,
One will attain the accomplishment of offering.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed One. Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment of the offering of the Tathāgata family:

When one is spent and weary from great joy,
That bliss arisen from great joy,
If one presents it as worship with the four mudrās,
Will swiftly bring success.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajradhara. Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment of the offering of the vajra family:

Having bound the commitment mudrā of hūṃ,
And having embraced a woman,
If one offers that one who is embraced,
It will be accomplished.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajra Hūṃ.

Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment of the offering of the lotus family:

Meditating on oneself as the Lord of the World,
And gazing upon the face of the beloved,
The bliss that arises from that gazing,
If one offers it, will be accomplished.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Padmanetra. Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment of the offering of the jewel family:

Having bound the karma mudrā,
And being adorned with every ornament,
The delight of embracing a woman,
If offered in worship, will be accomplished.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajraratna. This is the extensive tantra of the rite of the secret accomplishment of the offering of all the families.

Then the Great Bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of the secret offering of all the tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the secret offering of the Tathāgata family:

Meditating on oneself as a buddha,
In such a way that no one at all perceives it,
If one sports with another's woman,
One will attain the accomplishment of offering.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed One. Here, this is the tantra of the secret offering of the Tathāgata:

Having performed the vajra charm,
Binding on the vajra garland,
Then singing the vajra song,
And worshiping with the vajra dance:
This is the supreme empowerment through the joy of desire.
Apart from the bliss of song and dance,
No other bliss is experienced;
Therefore this supreme secret is unsurpassed.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the secret offering of the vajra family:

Meditating on Trailokyavijaya,
In the manner of the rite, exactly as prescribed,
If one worships with the secret offering,
One will attain the accomplishment of offering.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajraguhya. Here, this is the tantra of the secret accomplishment of the offering of the lotus family:

Abiding in the mudrā of taming beings,
Exactly in accordance with the secret way,
If one worships fully with the karma mudrā,
One will attain the accomplishment of offering.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the noble Samantāvalokiteśvara. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the secret offering of the jewel family:

Through the yoga of Sarvārthasiddhi,
Exactly in accordance with the secret way,
If one worships fully with the karma mudrā,
One will attain the accomplishment of offering.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajragarbha. This is the extensive tantra of the rite of the secret offering of all the families.

Then the Great Bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this tantra of the secret accomplishment of generating the families of all the tathāgatas. Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment of generating the reality of the Tathāgata:

In brief, if from the wisdom of absorption,
By way of passion for sentient beings,
One comes to accomplish buddhahood itself,
One will swiftly attain the unsurpassed.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Tathāgata. Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment of generating the great reality of the Tathāgata family:

Sentient beings are without beginning or end,
Having the defining character of the element of space.
Samantabhadra, the self of all,
Is the lord of all beings of desire.
That which is the mind of all sentient beings,
In order to teach it, is called the sattva, the being.
If one rests in equipoise in vajra evenness,
One has surely become the vajra itself;
By way of the blessing of the sattva,
One has become Vajrasattva himself.
That very one is the Blessed One, the sattva;
Vajrahāsa is the Tathāgata.
Fully realizing one's own mind and the rest,
In accordance with the rite of the awakening of the buddhas,
That very one, the Blessed Tathāgata,
Becomes the family of all.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Sattva without Beginning or End.

That itself is the Tathāgata family;
That itself is the great vajra family;
That itself is the pure lotus family;
That itself is the supreme jewel family.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Wheel of All Tathāgatas. This is the extensive tantra of the rite of the secret accomplishment of generating all the families.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of naming the mudrās of the families of all the tathāgatas. Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment of naming the signs of the Tathāgata family:

That very one, the Blessed One, the sattva,
Vajrasattva, abides in the heart.
From equipoise comes absorption,
And one fully accomplishes the awakening of the buddhas.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed One. Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment of naming the signs of the Tathāgata family:

Because it fully realizes, it is the vajra;
By way of seizing, it is the hook;
Because it realizes the subtle, it is the arrow;
Because one is pleased, it is called "Excellent";
And because it adorns, it is called the jewel;
By way of splendor, it is the sun;
The banner is said to be raised up well;
Because of smiling, it is known as laughter;
Because desire is pure, it is the lotus;
The sword cuts down the enemy, the afflictions;
In the manner of a maṇḍala, it is the wheel;
Because it is uttered in words, it is called speech;
In the manner of a crossed vajra, it is the universal vajra;
By way of being unassailable, it is the armor;
By way of terrifying, it is the fangs;
By way of the mudrā, it is the clasp.
Just as the mind of the Blessed One is constant,
Vajrasattva, who pervades all,
So, if one meditates on the vajra signs and the rest,
One will swiftly succeed.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Sign of All Tathāgatas. This is the secret tantra of the accomplishment of naming the signs of all the families.

Then the Great Bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this secret tantra of the accomplishment of the arising of the mudrā binding of the families of all the tathāgatas. Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment by which the mudrā binding of the Tathāgata family arises:

Abiding in the absorption of Vajrasattva,
Assuming the vajra cross-legged posture,
And clasping the two hands in the vajra,
One will swiftly accomplish buddhahood itself.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed One.

Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment by which the mudrā binding of the Tathāgata family arises:

Just as a king's own seal
Is set upon the king's laws,
So the great-souled ones,
In the manner of an image
Of the vajra of body, speech and mind,
Set their own seal. If one knows this
As the great seal of the great-souled,
One will attain accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Great Mudrā. Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment by which the mudrā binding of the vajra family arises:

Just as the supreme commitment,
Once a person has bound himself to it,
Must never be transgressed,
So, by binding the commitment mudrā
Of all the tathāgatas,
If one binds the supreme vajra binding,
And knows not to transgress it until death,
One will attain accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajrasamaya. Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment by which the mudrā binding of the Dharma family arises:

In brief, the vajra command of the Victor
Must not be transgressed.
Knowing this to be the binding,
Through that comes the accomplishment of Dharma.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajradharma. Here, this is the secret tantra of the accomplishment by which the mudrā binding of the vajra karma family arises:

The vajra commands and the many actions
Must not be transgressed.
If one knows that action of heeding the command,
It will swiftly be accomplished.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajrasattva. This is the extensive secret tantra of the rite of the accomplishment by which the mudrā binding of all the families arises.

Then the Great Bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi said this to the Blessed One: "Blessed One, this too I have fully taken up and blessed."

Then again all the tathāgatas gathered together as one and gave their "Excellent!" to the Blessed One, the lord of all the tathāgatas, Vajrapāṇi:

The unsurpassed Vajra Vehicle,
The secret of all the tathāgatas,
This sūtra in which the Great Vehicle is gathered:
Because you have explained it well,
Excellent are you, Vajrasattva!
Vajraratna, excellent are you!
Vajradharma, excellent are you!
Vajrakarma, excellent are you!

From the Compendium of the Reality of All Tathāgatas, the extensive tantra of the secret rite of the procedures of all the families is complete.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of the holy Dharma of all the tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the holy Dharma of the Tathāgata family:

By way of recollecting the buddhas,
If one meditates in equipoise
On the absorption of the Dharma of the buddhas,
One will attain the unsurpassed accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Tathāgata. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the holy Dharma of the Tathāgata family:

By way of recollecting with passion,
Abiding in the absorption of all the sattvas,
If one meditates in perfect equipoise,
One will attain the supreme accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Absorption of All Tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the holy Dharma of the vajra family:

Meditating in the guise of Trailokyavijaya,
And likewise before oneself,
Having come to the power of Vajrapāṇi,
One will be victorious over the three realms.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajrasattva. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the holy Dharma of the lotus family:

Meditating as the Dharma that tames beings,
And likewise before oneself,
Endowed with the supreme of all aspects,
One fully carries out the taming.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajradharma. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the holy Dharma of the jewel family:

Meditating, too, on the body of Sarvārthasiddhi,
And likewise before oneself,
Endowed with all the supreme aspects,
Through that one attains perfect aims.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajramaṇi. This is the extensive tantra of the rite of the accomplishment of the holy Dharma of all the families.

Then the Great Bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of the Dharma commitment of the families of all the tathāgatas. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the Dharma commitment of all the tathāgatas:

Recollecting the buddhas,
In the manner of the subtle vajra,
If one says vajra vajra,
One will attain the accomplishment of the buddhas.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed One. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the Dharma commitment of the Tathāgata family:

In the absorption of Vajrasattva,
Meditating on the subtle vajra,
If one says vajra satva,
One accomplishes Vajrasattva himself.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajrasattva. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the Dharma commitment of the vajra family:

In the guise of Trailokyavijaya,
One recollects, and likewise before oneself;
If one says hūṃ hūṃ hūṃ hūṃ,
One will attain the unsurpassed accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajra Hūṃ. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the Dharma commitment of the lotus family:

In the form of the tamer of beings,
Meditating on the subtle vajra,
If one says siddhya siddhya,
One will attain the supreme accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajradharma. Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the Dharma commitment of the jewel family:

In the form of Arthasiddhi,
Having meditated on the subtle vajra,
If one says siddhya siddhya,
One will attain the supreme accomplishment of aims.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Jewel Dharma. This is the extensive tantra of the rite of the accomplishment of the Dharma commitment of all the families.

Then the Great Bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the holy Dharma of all the tathāgatas.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the holy Dharma of all the tathāgatas:

The holy Dharma is without letters;
It is the source of the wisdom of absorption.
Through the syllable A, all phenomena
Are understood to be unarisen.
Meditating with understanding
In the manner of this mudrā, and then,
With the wisdom of all letters,
One becomes a sugata in an instant.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the noble Mañjuśrī, who is all the tathāgatas.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the holy Dharma of the tathāgata family:

If, with faith, having come to trust it,
One holds or recites this sūtra
Of the reality of all the tathāgatas,
One will attain the unsurpassed accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajrasattva.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the holy Dharma of the vajra family:

For the benefit of sinful beings,
To turn back the evil conduct of the wicked,
And to protect the word of the Buddha,
If one carries out killing, one will succeed.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajradhara.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the holy Dharma of the lotus family:

Knowing attachment to be pure by nature,
And contemplating it as the ultimate meaning,
If one meditates on this at the beginning and at the end,
One will attain the accomplishment of Dharma.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the noble Avalokiteśvara.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of knowing the Dharma of the jewel family:

Giving for the sake of all sentient beings,
And fulfilling every wish,
One who meditates on this Dharma
Will attain the accomplishment of the jewel family.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Dharmaratna.

This is the extensive tantra of the rite of the wisdom of the holy Dharma of all the families.

Then the Great Bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of the action of the absorption of all the tathāgatas.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the action of absorption of the tathāgata family:

Through the absorption of the buddhas,
The buddhas fully accomplished awakening.
Having meditated on this,
One will attain the supreme accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajradhātu.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the action of absorption of the tathāgata family:

Through the absorption of Vajrasattva,
The unsurpassed and the many actions,
And all the transformations of all the sattvas,
If one knows them, will also be accomplished.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajrasattva.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the action of absorption of the vajra family:

So that sin may be purified,
If those who commit the deeds of immediate retribution cannot be turned back,
Then, when one has faith in the act of killing,
By that one will succeed.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajrasattva.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the action of absorption of the lotus family:

The self wholly purified of all sins:
Because all is pure, every purpose
Is accomplished by this action.
If one knows this, one will also succeed.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Padma.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the action of absorption of the jewel family:

Ever fulfilling all the wishes
Of all the poor who are in craving,
Through Sarvārthasiddhi, the lord of all
Will be accomplished; of this there is no doubt.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Ratnaketu.

This is the tantra of the accomplishment of the action of absorption of all the families.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of the subtle wisdom of all the tathāgata families.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the subtle wisdom of the tathāgata family:

If one always applies oneself with every effort
In the manner of the subtle vajra,
One will attain the five superknowledges
Endowed with all the supreme aspects.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed One.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the subtle wisdom of the tathāgata family:

Before others who are not the master,
Before those who are not disciples and not sons,
One should not speak of the mudrā;
By keeping them secret, the mudrā is accomplished.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajradhara.

Here, this is the tantra of the wisdom of the subtle accomplishment of the vajra family:

In the manner of the subtle vajra,
Being endowed with hūṃ arising from the nose,
Through the absorption of the vajra wrathful one
One accomplishes every purpose.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajra.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the subtle wisdom of the lotus family:

Abiding in the absorption of the Great Lotus,
In the manner of the subtle vajra,
If one gazes with the vajra gaze,
One will attain the accomplishment one desires.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Blossoming Lotus.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the subtle wisdom of the jewel family:

Through the absorption of Vajraratna,
In the manner of the subtle vajra,
With an extremely subtle, blazing gaze,
One draws in every kind of wealth.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajramaṇi.

This is the tantra of the subtle accomplishment of all the families.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the eye of all the tathāgatas.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the eye of the tathāgata:

When the mudrā or the absorption
Has been bestowed for the sake of accomplishment,
When one sees, like stars in the sky,
White lights darting about,
Then the wise one knows,
"I shall obtain the eye of the buddhas."
From then on, one should practice
Through all the rites of the buddhas.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed One.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the eye of the tathāgata family:

Places and beings likewise,
However many things there may be,
If one sees the reflections of them all
Darting through the sky, going or coming,
And, knowing them as the pure vajra eye,
Makes offering, one will attain
The unsurpassed accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajrasattva.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the eye of the vajra family:

If, to the right or to the left,
In the sky, the very sphere of space
Is seen by the eye
Swiftly whirling like clouds,
And having seen the mudrā arisen from the vajra
One does not become afraid,
If one then takes hold of that mudrā,
The vajra eye will be accomplished.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajra.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the eye of the lotus family:

When one comes to see a maṇḍala
That is grey or red,
White or yellow or of many colors,
Then, as intended, the eye of the wheel will be accomplished.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajrapadma.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the eye of the jewel family:

When one sees the sky
As like jewels,
And like gold and the rest,
That eye of the sky will be accomplished.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajragarbha.

This is the extensive tantra of the rite of the accomplishment of the wisdom of the eye of all the families.

Then the Great Bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of the holy action of all the tathāgata families.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the holy action of the tathāgata family:

In the manner of the Dharma maṇḍala,
For the ripening of the bliss of attachment,
One who has worshipped all the lords
Will attain the accomplishment of a buddha.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed One.

Here, this is the tantra of the holy accomplishment of the tathāgata family:

Meditating, "I am attachment,"
If one thoroughly performs the secret offering,
One will attain the supreme accomplishment,
Equal to the radiant vajra of desire.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajrahāsa.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the holy action of the vajra family:

One who meditates, "I am wrath,"
And thoroughly performs the secret offering,
Will attain the supreme accomplishment
That is like Trailokyavijaya.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the noble Vajrapāṇi.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the holy action of the Dharma family:

With a mind turned inward,
Meditating on desire as supreme,
If with syllables joined to melody
One worships the buddhas,
One will swiftly attain accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the noble Samantāvalokiteśvara.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the holy action of the jewel family:

Resting in equipoise as Vajragarbha,
Bowing down with a stirred intention,
If one always strives at prostration,
One will obtain empowerment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Precious Vajra Empowerment.

This is the extensive tantra of the rite of the accomplishment of the holy action of all the families.

Then the Great Bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this tantra of the secret accomplishment of the commitment of action of all the tathāgatas.

Here, this is the tantra of the secret accomplishment of the commitment of the tathāgata family:

All the bliss of desire and the rest
Abides always in my heart.
How wonderful! Marvelous is what is taught
As protecting the aims of sentient beings.
This is the commitment of action:
Having meditated in the absorption of the tathāgata,
If one worships the buddhas,
One will attain the supreme accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed One.

Here, this is the tantra of the secret accomplishment of the commitment of action of the tathāgata family:

Abiding in the absorption of Vajrasattva,
I am the all-good Desire
Who gives bliss to all sentient beings;
If one worships, one will attain accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Kāma.

Here, this is the tantra of the secret accomplishment of the commitment of the vajra family:

I am the all-good Wrathful One
Who benefits all sentient beings;
If one worships through the yoga of Vajra Hūṃ,
One will attain accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajrapāṇi.

Here, this is the tantra of the secret accomplishment of the commitment of action of the lotus family:

I am the all-good Desire
Who bestows every bliss;
Abiding oneself in the form of the Tamer of Beings,
If one worships, one will attain accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Lotus of Passion.

Here, this is the tantra of the secret accomplishment of the commitment of action of the jewel family:

I am the all-good King
Who gives great wealth to all sentient beings;
If one worships in the form of Sarvārthasiddhi,
One will attain accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, Accomplishment of All Aims.

This is the extensive tantra of the rite of the secret accomplishment of the commitment of action of all the families.

Then the Great Bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of the holy Dharma of action of all the tathāgata families.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the nature of reality of action of the tathāgata:

Holding one's own absorption,
Having tied a knot with the right finger,
One binds it on as armor and places it on the crown;
One will then be exceedingly well protected.

Thus spoke the Buddha, the Blessed One.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the nature of reality of action of the action family of the tathāgata:

Having drawn a picture on a wall
Of a likeness, and raised the sign,
When it is pleased, whatever woman one has thought of
Will come under one's power.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the noble Vajrapāṇi.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the nature of reality of action of the vajra family:

Having drawn the face of a yakṣa on the ground,
If one plants a fingernail
At the place of its eyes,
One will draw in the finest of women.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajrasattva.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the nature of reality of action of the lotus family:

Taking a lotus in both hands,
And contemplating attachment as pure,
If one looks at a woman with the vajra gaze,
She too will be delighted.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Padmadhara.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the nature of reality of action of the jewel family:

Abiding in the absorption of Vajraratna,
Holding a jewel in both hands,
In the manner of hūṃ,
One makes all women bow down.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajra Hūṃ.

This is the extensive tantra of the rite of the accomplishment of action of all the families.

Then the Great Bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of the purposes of action of all the tathāgatas.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the purposes of action of the tathāgata family:

Applying oneself to the rite of the action of offering,
Whatever purpose one has in mind,
Praying for each and every one,
The wise one should accomplish the mudrā.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajradhātu.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the purposes of action of the tathāgata family:

Through the absorption of Vajrasattva,
Applying oneself to the rite of the secret offering,
Whatever purpose one speaks of,
One will swiftly attain its accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajradharma.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the purposes of action of the vajra family:

Having entered the absorption of the wrathful one,
And made the great secret offering of the family,
Whatever the wise one has in mind, however small,
Will swiftly be accomplished.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajrakrodha.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the purposes of action of the lotus family:

Through the absorption of the Lord of the World,
Having made offering with the mind,
Whatever purpose the wise one has in mind,
He obtains all its accomplishments.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Padmadhara.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the purposes of action of the jewel family:

Through the absorption of Vajragarbha,
Having made offerings of incense and the rest,
Whatever purpose the wise one has in mind,
All of it will be accomplished in an instant.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajragarbha.

This is the extensive tantra of the rite of the accomplishment of the purposes of action of all the families.

Then the Blessed One Vajrapāṇi spoke this tantra of the accomplishment of meditation on the mudrās of all the tathāgatas.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the yoga of the blessing of the tathāgata:

Through the absorption of buddha yoga,
In the manner of the subtle vajra,
By fully accomplishing the buddha mudrā,
One will attain the supreme accomplishment.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the Tathāgata Vajrapāṇi.

Here, this is the tantra of the accomplishment of the yoga of the blessing of the sattva of the tathāgata family:

The sattva who is the self of all
Abides within my body.
Blessing oneself with "I am the sattva,"
If one meditates with the pride of "I,"
One will succeed.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajrasattva of the Full Realization of the Great Vehicle of all the tathāgatas.

Here, this is the tantra of the yoga of the vajra blessing of the vajra family:

As the sattva is, so is the mudrā;
As the mudrā is, so am I.
In the manner of this rite,
One should accomplish all the mudrās.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajradhara.

Here, this is the tantra of the yoga of the blessing by Dharma of the lotus family:

In the manner of the Dharma mudrā,
Having meditated on the subtle vajra,
Through the mudrā of speech, all of these
Become the absorption of the great sattva.

Thus spoke the Blessed One, the noble Samantāvalokiteśvara.

Here, this is the tantra of the yoga of the blessing by action of the jewel family:

Empowerment for all the buddhas,
Offerings, commitments and accomplishments:
Having meditated on these as the Blessed One,
One should accomplish the vajra actions.

Thus spoke the Blessed One Vajrakarma.

This is the tantra of the accomplishment of meditation on the mudrās of all the families.

Then again all the blessed tathāgatas gathered together as one and gave their "Excellent!" to the Blessed One, the bodhisattva, the great being, the wheel-turning sovereign of all the tathāgatas, Vajrapāṇi:

Because you have well explained
The unsurpassed Vajra Vehicle,
The secret of all the tathāgatas,
This sūtra of the Full Realization of the Great Vehicle,
Excellent, Vajrasattva, are you!
Vajraratna, you are excellent!
Vajradharma, you are excellent,
And Vajrakarma, you are excellent!

From the Compendium of the Reality of All Tathāgatas, the extensive rite of the secret later tantra of all the ritual systems is complete.

The ninth fascicle.

Then the bodhisattva, the great being Vajrapāṇi, spoke this verse:

To those of wrong views and devoid of passion,
This secret is not fitting.
For the benefit of all sentient beings,
I shall explain the rite accordingly.

Then again the bodhisattva Vajrapāṇi spoke this extensive tantra of the requisites and rites of all the tathāgata families.

Here, this is the extensive tantra of the requisites and rites of all the tathāgata families. Here, this is the extensive tantra of the requisites and rites of the heart: having beheld the great maṇḍala, one makes offerings with the mudrās of incense, lamp, flower and scent, and then one should begin recitation with the vajra words.

Here, this is the extensive tantra of the rite of recitation: remaining for four months in the proper manner, and having made offerings at the four times of day with incense and the rest in accordance with the rite, one praises all the tathāgatas with the hundred and eight names of the realization of the Great Vehicle of all the tathāgatas. Having paid homage by way of prostration with the four mudrās, one should offer one's own body and make offering with these four mantras.

oṃ sarba tathāgata pūjopasthānāya ātmbanaṃ niryātayāmi
sarba tathāgata bajra satvā tiṣṭha sva māṃ
oṃ sarba tathāgata pūjā abhiṣekāya ātmānaṃ niryātayāmi
sarba tathāgata bajra ratna abhiṣiñca māṃ
oṃ sarba tathāgata phūja prabartanāya ātmānaṃ nirayātayāmi
sarba tathāgata najra ṅharma prabartaya māṃ
oṃ sarba tathāgata pūjā karmane ātmānaṃ niryātayāmi
sarba tathāgata bajra karma kuru māṃ

Then, not parted from attachment to all the qualities of desire, enjoying every food and every pleasure, one meditates for the sake of one's own heart before an image of the Buddha, even if only with words, and does as one pleases.
